A taxonomy organizes the literature through four LLM attachment points: policy, reward design, communication between agents, and hierarchical planning. A conditional capability profile separates native mechanism, reported performance, formal guarantee, and engineering maturity, and a deployment readiness analysis identifies the evidence behind each role. These stages yield the principal contribution: a three layer MARL centered reference architecture, grounded in evidence, for semantic reasoning, adaptive cooperative control, and independently assured execution. The LLM-Augmented Dec-POMDP is a descriptive comparative notation for that architecture, recording four attachment choices without introducing a new decision process class or algorithm. Under the reviewed evidence, conventional MARL is better suited to frequent, structured, decentralized coordination after task specific training, whereas LLM components are promising for semantic interpretation, reward drafting, human interaction, and slower supervisory planning. Current LLM only manufacturing controllers do not yet establish equivalence for strict real time, decentralized, safety critical control; this conclusion is bounded by the available evidence and does not assert impossibility.

</details>


### [65] [NiyamAI - An Intent-Bound AI Agent with Cryptographically Verifiable Guardrails using Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07167)
*Aditya Katkar,Om Karkele,Kartik Mandhane,Manisha More,Yash Kashid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Niyam-AI 是一个使LLM智能体安全强制执行可验证的框架：通过将允许的工具和约束锁定为SHA-256承诺的意图合约（Intent Contract），由隔离的Judge模型拦截和验证每个工具调用，并通过EZKL生成zk-SNARK证明后工具才执行。在Agent-SafetyBench的2,000个真实场景中取得88.5% F1分数和1.1%误报率，显著优于多个基线，同时增加约2.26秒的证明生成开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体面临提示注入、幻觉推理和不安全工具调用等主要攻击面，现有防御依赖运行在被攻击同一机器上的系统提示或策略过滤器，缺乏可验证的执行证明。作者旨在提供一种既能保证安全又能提供数学上可验证的证明的防护框架。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：(1) 会话开始时将允许的工具和约束锁定为通过SHA-256提交的意图合约；(2) 每个工具调用由隔离的Judge模型拦截和验证；(3) 通过EZKL生成zk-SNARK证明，工具仅在证明验证后执行，允许第三方无需访问Judge模型权重即可确认强制执行。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench的2,000个场景中，Niyam-AI达到88.5% F1分数和1.1%误报率（bootstrap 95% CI: [85.19%, 91.88%]）。McNemar检验显示显著优于NeMo Guardrails（390胜/20负）、Llama Prompt Guard 2（115胜/13负）和GPT-OSS-Safeguard（384胜/19负），p<0.0001。证明生成耗时2260.6±218.4ms，验证耗时53.1±11.8ms。

Conclusion: Niyam-AI提供了一种既高度准确又数学上可验证的护栏机制，其零知识证明范式让第三方能独立确认安全强制执行的真实性。但作者也坦承分类器是针对Agent-SafetyBench适应训练的，且基线的零样本评估使对比不完全对等，这一局限性在Section IV.C中讨论。

Abstract: Giving an AI agent the ability to send emails, query databases, or execute commands is useful--until the agent is tricked into doing something it shouldn't. Prompt injection, hallucinated reasoning, and unsafe tool calls form the primary attack surface for autonomous LLM agents. Existing defenses rely on software checks like system prompts or policy filters running on the same machine the attacker targets, offering no verifiable proof of execution. We introduce Niyam-AI, a framework that makes safety enforcement provable. At session start, permitted tools and constraints are locked into an Intent Contract committed via SHA-256. Every tool call is intercepted and validated by an isolated Judge model; upon passing, a zk-SNARK proof is generated via EZKL.
  The tool executes only after proof verification, allowing third parties to confirm enforcement without accessing Judge model weights. Evaluating Niyam-AI on 2,000 real-world scenarios from Agent-SafetyBench against NeMo Guardrails, Meta's Llama Prompt Guard 2, and OpenAI's GPT-OSS-Safeguard using 5-fold stratified cross-validation yields an F1 score of 88.5% with a 1.1% false-positive rate (bootstrap 95% CI: [85.19%, 91.88%], N=1000). McNemar's exact paired test confirms significant improvement: Niyam-AI wins 390 discordant scenarios against NeMo (vs 20 losses), 115 against Prompt Guard 2 (vs 13), and 384 against GPT-OSS-Safeguard (vs 19) with p < 0.0001 in all cases.
  Proof generation adds 2260.6 +/- 218.4 ms per approved action, while verification takes 53.1 +/- 11.8 ms. Niyam-AI provides a guardrail that is both highly accurate and mathematically verifiable--though this reflects a classifier adapted to Agent-SafetyBench evaluated against zero-shot baselines, a distinction discussed in Section IV.C.

</details>


### [66] [Agent Memory Distillation: Empowering Small LLM Agents with Hierarchical Teacher Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07169)
*Taeil Kim,Kangsan Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Memory Distillation (AMD)，一种无需训练的框架，通过层级记忆将大型教师agent的结构化知识迁移给小型学生agent，在三个工具使用基准上显著提升4B-8B学生模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统已被证明能提升agent性能，但对于小型语言模型而言，其潜力尚未被充分探索。小模型难以自行生成足够多的成功轨迹，需要一种方法来利用大模型的知识提升小模型的工具使用能力。

Method: AMD从教师agent的成功轨迹中构建三种互补的记忆类型：Workflow memory编码任务级策略，Subtask memory提供中间粒度的具体行为示例，Function memory捕获函数调用约定和常见陷阱。Workflow和Subtask记忆在任务开始时主动注入，而Function记忆在工具调用出错时被动检索。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准（AppWorld、BFCL V3、ToolSandbox）上，使用四个4B-8B学生模型和GPT-5-mini教师模型，分别获得27.2%p、11.2%p和3.4%p的平均准确率提升，且一致优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。分析显示Subtask记忆贡献最大，教师效果取决于教师能力和学生兼容性，4B规模的学生受益最多。

Conclusion: AMD是一种有效的训练-free知识蒸馏框架，通过层级记忆将教师知识迁移给小模型，显著提升其工具使用性能，且无需额外训练成本。

Abstract: Memory systems have shown promise for improving agent performance, but their potential remains largely unexplored for small language models, which struggle to generate sufficient successful trajectories on their own. We propose Agent Memory Distillation (AMD), a training-free framework that transfers structured knowledge from a large teacher agent to a small student agent through hierarchical memory. AMD constructs three complementary memory types from successful teacher trajectories: Workflow memory encodes task-level strategies, Subtask memory provides concrete behavioral examples at an intermediate granularity, and Function memory captures per-function calling conventions and common pitfalls. Workflow and Subtask memories are injected proactively at the start of each task, while Function memory is retrieved reactively upon tool-calling errors. We evaluate AMD on three tool-use benchmarks using four student models (4B-8B parameters) with GPT-5-mini as the teacher, achieving average accuracy gains of 27.2%p, 11.2%p, and 3.4%p on AppWorld, BFCL V3, and ToolSandbox, while consistently outperforming existing memory-based baselines. Further analysis shows that Subtask memory contributes the largest gains, teacher effectiveness depends on both teacher capability and student compatibility, and 4B-sized students benefit most from AMD.

</details>


### [67] [SetEasy: A Multi-Modal Classroom Engagement Assessment and Seating Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07188)
*Zhihao Xie,Hongye Yang,Shien Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SetEasy系统通过融合多模态感知（腕带生理数据、4K视频、环境数据）训练v-Gage模型评估课堂参与度，并利用CP-SAT优化器在固定座位网格中生成满足视觉可达性和社交动态约束的每周座位安排。在4周、23名学生的331节课部署中，参与度预测RMSE从0.75降至0.53，平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，验证了无需硬件改动即可显著提升课堂参与度的可解释数据驱动座位策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定座位网格中课堂参与度不均衡、后排低参与模式普遍等问题，探索无需硬件改造、可解释且可持续的数据驱动座位优化方法。在全球教育同质化背景下，需要一种可迁移、文化响应的差异化空间设计范式来替代凭直觉排座的传统做法。

Method: SetEasy框架包含两大核心模块：(1) 多模态感知与评估：融合腕带生理信号、4K视频和环境数据，基于修订后的ISEQ（参与度分类体系）训练v-Gage模型，对情感、行为、认知和总体四个维度进行课堂参与度预测；(2) 双层优化：每周基于两周参与度预测构建学生-座位效用矩阵，使用CP-SAT求解器在视觉可达性和社交动态双重约束下生成最优座位计划。

Result: 在四周期限的部署实验（23名学生、331节课）中，v-Gage模型在情感、行为、认知和总体各维度上均收敛，预测RMSE从0.75降至0.53；优化后的座位安排使平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，超过三分之二的座位达到高参与度水平，且后排低活动模式显著减少。

Conclusion: 研究证明，无需任何硬件层面改动，可解释的、数据驱动的座位安排策略即能显著增强课堂参与度。多模态"评估+优化"范式为全球教育环境提供了可迁移、可持续的路径，支持文化响应的差异化空间教学设计，有望缓解教育空间设计中的同质化趋势。

Abstract: SetEasy optimizes classroom engagement in fixed seating grids. It fuses multimodal sensing (wristband physiology, 4K video, environmental data) and trains a v-Gage model grounded in a revised ISEQ. Each week, two-week engagement forecasts are mapped to a student-seat utility matrix, and CP-SAT generates seating plans under visual-access and social-dynamics constraints. In a four-week deployment (23 students, 331 classes), v-Gage converged across affective, behavioral, cognitive, and overall dimensions, cutting RMSE from 0.75 to 0.53. Optimization raised mean engagement from 0.30 to 0.70, with over two-thirds of seats reaching high engagement and back-row low-activity patterns markedly reduced. These results show that, without hardware changes, interpretable, data-driven seating strategies can substantially enhance engagement. The multimodal "assessment + optimization" paradigm offers a transferable, sustainable path to culturally responsive, differentiated spatial design amid global homogenization.

</details>


### [68] [EMAS: Stabilizing Multi-Agent System Evolution through Evidence-Guided Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07196)
*Chao Fei,Qingyi Si,Kaihua Liang,Yanghua Xiao,Panos Kalnis,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EMAS是一种通过将推理轨迹转化为结构化诊断来持续演化多智能体系统拓扑和提示词的方法，能够在两个演化周期内显著提升准确率并大幅降低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统自动化设计方法通常在初始设计阶段优化提示词和拓扑结构后便固定部署，无法从后续样本中积累经验并转化为可复用的系统更新，同时追求高准确率的设计往往带来高昂的token成本。

Method: EMAS将智能体运行轨迹转化为结构化诊断，每条诊断指定一个修订操作及其目标对象。只有当相同诊断在多个样本中重复出现时才生成候选修订，并且只有在对当前MAS进行配对验证且满足相应接受标准时才实际应用该修订。整个过程无需更新LLM参数，通过调整MAS拓扑和提示词实现演化。

Result: 在四个基准测试和两个LLM上，EMAS在两个backbone上均取得最高的任务加权总体准确率，在八个模型-基准组合中有六个达到最佳或并列最佳。在Kimi-K2-6和Qwen3.6-27B上分别在两个演化周期内取得6.30%和20.10%的相对准确率提升。在Qwen3.6-27B的MBPP任务上，准确率从55.09%提升至89.12%，同时每任务token使用量降低62.2%。

Conclusion: EMAS证明可以将新样本产生的经验转化为MAS拓扑和提示词的可复用更新，同时兼顾准确率提升和成本降低，是一种有效的多智能体系统持续演化方法。

Abstract: Many methods for automated multi-agent system design optimize prompts and topologies during an initial design stage and then deploy the resulting system unchanged on subsequent samples. Experience from these samples is rarely consolidated into reusable system updates, while accuracy-oriented designs may incur high token costs. We introduce EMAS (Evolving Multi-Agent System), which uses this experience to revise MAS topology and prompts without updating LLM parameters, either to improve accuracy or to reduce cost. EMAS converts traces into structured diagnoses that specify a revision operation and target. It generates a candidate revision only when the same diagnosis recurs across samples and applies it only if paired validation against the current MAS meets the corresponding acceptance criterion. Across four benchmarks and two LLMs, EMAS attains the highest task-weighted overall accuracy for both backbones and is best or tied in six of eight model--benchmark settings. Within two evolution epochs, EMAS achieves relative gains of 6.30% and 20.10% in task-weighted accuracy on Kimi-K2-6 and Qwen3.6-27B, respectively. On MBPP with Qwen3.6-27B, EMAS raises accuracy from 55.09% to 89.12% while reducing token use per task by 62.2%. These results show that EMAS can turn experience from new samples into reusable updates to MAS topology and prompts.

</details>


### [69] [Authoring and Management of Transparent Research Integrity Assessments of Randomised Clinical Trial Publications Using LLM-assisted Tools and Provenance Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07202)
*Milan Markovic,Goutham Indukuri,Somayajulu Sripada,Colby J. Vorland,Jack Wilkinson,Clare Robertson,Mark Bolland,Andrew Grey,Miriam Brazzelli,Alison Avenell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍INSPECT-AI，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的交互式工具，用于辅助人类审稿人对已发表的随机对照试验（RCT）进行研究诚信评估，基于社区认可的INSPECT-SR框架，并使用RIPE-O本体记录评估过程溯源。同时提出RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含95篇RCT的140个专家研究诚信评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCT的系统综述是临床护理指南的常用证据来源，但需满足高标准的研究诚信要求。然而，对已发表RCT进行研究诚信评估是一个复杂的手动过程，且不同人工评估者的意见可能存在分歧，因此需要工具来辅助提高评估效率和一致性。

Method: 开发了INSPECT-AI，一个基于LLM的交互式工具，基于INSPECT-SR框架协助人类审稿人进行RCT研究诚信评估；使用RIPE-O本体记录评估过程的溯源信息；构建RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含140个来自95篇RCT出版物的专家研究诚信评估。

Result: 生成了RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含由INSPECT-AI生成的140个专家级研究诚信评估，覆盖95篇RCT出版物，并使用RIPE-O本体进行描述。

Conclusion: INSPECT-AI为RCT研究诚信评估提供了LLM辅助的交互式解决方案，结合社区框架和溯源本体，旨在提高评估过程的透明性和可追溯性，最终支持高质量临床护理指南的制定。

Abstract: Systematic reviews of Randomised Controlled Trials (RCTs) are routinely used as evidence for clinical care guidelines. Such evidence has to meet high research integrity standards to prevent low quality or false research outputs influencing the clinical care. However, assessing research integrity of published RCTs is a complex process requiring manual effort, and potentially resulting in diverse opinions of the human assessors. This paper describes INSPECT-AI, an LLM-based interactive tool that assists human reviewers with research integrity assessments of published RCTs based on the community approved INSPECT-SR framework, and the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology (RIPE-O) for documenting the provenance of the assessment process. In addition, we present the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence knowledge graph (RIPE-KG), an initial set of 140 expert research integrity assessments of 95 RCT publications generated by INSPECT-AI and described using RIPE-O.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond the Black Box: Interpretable Models of Human Randomisation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07220)
*Ngoc Linh Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了O'Neill零和纸牌游戏中人类玩家偏离混合策略均衡的行为。使用84,060个决策数据（2,802对玩家），比较了朴素模型、行为模型、可解释机器学习与深度学习模型的预测能力，发现重复/回避行为（尤其是玩家管理自身近期行动历史）解释了大部分可解释且可利用的策略信号，而频率追踪模型在样本外几乎没有增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合策略均衡预测随机独立同分布的行动，但人类玩家系统性地偏离这一基准，且这一偏离可以被LSTM等黑盒序列模型预测。作者希望找到透明的替代模型，既能解释行为结构，又能保持预测能力，从而揭示人类决策背后的行为机制。

Method: 使用84,060个来自2,802对玩家的决策数据，首先将朴素模型和行为模型与可解释机器学习及深度学习模型进行基准对比，然后评估先前研究中的改进EWA（Experience Weighted Attraction）规格，并利用LASSO诊断进一步提出嵌套的频率追踪扩展模型。

Result: 重复或回避行为，尤其是玩家对自身近期行动历史的管理，构成了大部分可解释且策略上可利用的预测信号；频率追踪扩展模型在样本外预测上几乎没有额外贡献。

Conclusion: 透明的行为模型能够达到黑盒序列模型的大部分预测能力，核心信号来源于玩家对自身近期行动历史的重复/回避倾向，而更复杂的频率追踪机制未能提供显著的额外样本外预测价值。

Abstract: Mixed strategy equilibrium predicts i.i.d play: past actions should not help predict future decisions. Human players, however, systematically depart from this benchmark, and in O'Neill's zero sum card game, these departures can be predicted by black box sequence models such as LSTMs. This paper asks whether that predictive power can be achieved by transparent alternatives that also reveal the behavioural structure behind it. Using 84,060 decisions from 2,802 pairs, the analysis first benchmarks naive and behavioral models against interpretable machine learning and deep learning models, then evaluates the modified EWA specifications of prior work against these benchmarks and uses the LASSO diagnostics to motivate a further nested frequency tracking extension. The results show that repeat or avoid behavior, especially players' management of their own recent action histories, accounts for most of the interpretable and strategically exploitable signal, while frequency tracking adds little out of sample.

</details>


### [71] [From probability to causality in probabilistic logic programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07230)
*Zora Wurm,Kilian Rückschloß,Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究概率逻辑编程中因果顺序的可识别性。由于从数据中学习的程序仅利用概率信息，而单一概率分布可能兼容多种因果顺序，导致干预推理存在歧义。作者利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络的关系，推导出概率信息唯一确定因果顺序的条件，并通过关系词汇引入的因果对称性约束，给出验证学习到的概率逻辑程序是否支持良定义干预语义的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率逻辑编程是统计关系人工智能中支持因果查询（包括外部干预）的形式化方法。然而，当从数据中学习程序结构时，仅利用概率信息，而一个概率分布可能兼容多种因果顺序，导致干预推理产生歧义。因此需要回答何时因果顺序由分布唯一确定的问题。

Method: 利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的对应关系，推导出概率信息唯一决定因果顺序的充分条件；同时引入由底层关系词汇表所诱导的因果对称性约束，将这些约束纳入考量，最终形成一个验证方法。

Result: 提出了一种方法，用于验证从数据中学习到的概率逻辑程序是否具备良定义的干预语义，即其因果顺序是否被概率信息与关系结构约束唯一确定。

Conclusion: 通过结合贝叶斯网络的等价类理论与关系结构中的对称性约束，给出了概率逻辑程序中因果顺序可识别性的判定条件，为学习到的程序支持可靠的干预推理提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Probabilistic logic programming is a formalism of statistical relational artificial intelligence that supports causal queries, including interventions from outside the system. When the structure of a probabilistic logic program is learned from data, however, only probabilistic information is used, and a single probability distribution may be compatible with several causal orders. This leads to ambiguity in interventional reasoning, raising the question of when the causal order is uniquely determined by the distribution. Exploiting the relationship between acyclic probabilistic logic programs and Bayesian networks, we derive conditions under which the probabilistic information encoded in a program determines a unique causal order. We also incorporate constraints arising from relational structure by taking into account prescribed sets of causal symmetries induced by the underlying relational vocabulary. The result is a method for verifying when a learned probabilistic logic program supports well-defined intervention semantics.

</details>


### [72] [Recipes for Creativity: Iterative Generation and Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07243)
*Rens Anderson,Tessa Verhoef,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过将FunSearch改编为2024年Pillsbury烘焙大赛的食谱生成任务，探究迭代搜索是否能提升LLM创造力。结果表明迭代生成-选择可产生与人类基准相当的创意分数，但单纯增加迭代次数并不会提升创造力；循环内评估器的设计才是关键，较小的选择评分模型在多数TTCT维度上显著更优，而温度对创造力的影响有限（仅原始性维度例外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的评测往往基于单一产物，而人类创造力通常通过迭代生成、评估和优化的过程涌现。本研究旨在探讨迭代搜索是否能够提升LLM的创造力表现，从而弥补现有评估范式的不足。

Method: 将FunSearch方法改编应用于2024年Pillsbury烘焙大赛的食谱生成任务，使用基于TTCT的LLM评估方法，设计两个实验分别测试迭代次数、生成器温度以及循环内选择评分器模型大小对创造性输出的影响。

Result: 迭代生成-选择能够产生与人类基准相当的创造力分数；额外迭代次数本身不会提升创造力；循环内评估器（选择评分器）是最关键的设计变量，小型评分器在大多数TTCT维度上显著更优；温度仅在原始性维度上有有限影响。

Conclusion: 评估器的设计是主观创造性搜索中的一阶设计变量，其对创造力输出的影响远大于迭代次数和温度等参数，这一发现对生成式搜索系统的设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Generative models are often evaluated through singular artifacts, whereas human creativity typically emerges through iterative generation, appraisal, and refinement. This pilot study examines whether iterative search improves LLM creativity by adapting FunSearch to recipe generation for the 2024 Pillsbury Bake-Off and evaluating outputs against human benchmarks using TTCT-based LLM evaluation. Across two experiments, we test iteration count, generator temperature, and in-loop selection-scorer model size. Results show that iterative generation-selection can produce recipes with creativity scores comparable to human benchmarks, but additional iterations alone do not improve creativity. The in-loop evaluator matters most: a smaller selection scorer yields significantly higher scores across most TTCT dimensions, while temperature has limited effects except for originality. These findings suggest that evaluator design is a first-order design variable in subjective creative search.

</details>


### [73] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了A²E（Agent Auditing Engine），一个端到端的Agent harness评估引擎。通过新的Agent Task Protocol (ATP)实现不同harness的快速集成，利用自动插桩的Monitor捕获标准化执行轨迹，并在评估阶段使用多维度指标系统评估harness能力。实验发现模型-harness组合在不同任务类型上表现差异显著，没有单一组合能在所有任务上全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展，harness作为部署agent的关键基础设施日益重要，但如何高效构建端到端、系统化且全面的评估流水线仍是一个重大挑战。当前缺乏对agent harness能力的系统性评估方法，传统仅依赖正确率的评估无法细粒度刻画harness之间的差异。

Method: A²E采用三层架构：(1) 提出Agent Task Protocol (ATP)协议，统一任务描述格式以支持快速集成不同harness；(2) 通过自动插桩的Monitor在实验过程中捕获并生成标准化的执行轨迹；(3) 在评估阶段使用多维指标体系（执行效率、工具使用、任务规划、错误恢复等），比仅看正确率提供更细粒度的harness能力刻画。

Result: 实验表明模型-harness组合在不同类型任务上表现出显著性能差异，没有任何单一组合能在所有任务上一致超越其他组合，证实了系统化评估的必要性，并为模型与harness的共同演化提供了指导。

Conclusion: A²E提供了首个端到端、系统化的agent harness评估框架，填补了该领域评估工具的空白；实验结果强调了系统化评估agent harness的重要性，并为未来模型与harness的协同优化指明了方向。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [74] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QFCQT（量子-分形启发的混沌门控Quantformer），用于非平稳时间序列预测，通过Lee振荡器激活、Max-over-Time池化和平滑-混沌门控融合机制，在三个基准上超越Informer、LogTrans等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测面临长程依赖、局部波动突变、结构突变和非线性振荡行为等挑战。现有Transformer预测器的前馈块依赖平滑静态激活函数，对突变状态切换的敏感度不足，难以捕捉复杂的波动动态。

Method: QFCQT包含三个核心组件：(1) Quantformer风格的数值编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多变量输入；(2) 可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应，并通过Max-over-Time池化进行汇总；(3) 平滑-混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡常规平滑激活与混沌敏感响应。此外，采用八个参数化Lee振荡器族的软叠加来适应不同状态下的非线性响应模式。这里的"量子-分形"仅是计算类比，非严格物理推导。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股指三个基准上的实验表明，QFCQT一致性地优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线模型。

Conclusion: 融合振荡器激活与混沌门控机制的QFCQT能有效增强Transformer前馈层对非平稳、突变时序的建模能力，为复杂波动环境下的鲁棒预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [75] [Curriculum as Code: An AI-Assisted Architecture for Instructional Design in STEM Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07364)
*Henrique Mohallem Paiva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"课程即代码"(Curriculum as Code)范式的六阶段AI辅助教学设计架构，将生成式AI与LaTeX和Python集成，实现STEM教育中可复现、视觉一致且技术精确的教学材料自动化创建。经过一年在8个模块和28个项目情境中的验证，显著减少教师工作量，材料质量评分达8.5-9.9/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为主动学习创建定制化教学材料给教师带来沉重负担；标准演示工具对技术内容的支持不足；现有AI应用常产生幻觉且未能规范化教学编写流程，限制了其在严谨学术设计中的应用。

Method: 采用六阶段流水线架构，以文本驱动接口和代码驱动生成（LaTeX/Beamer生成幻灯片，Python生成图形）替代即兴的提示工程，由教学约束、情境校准和自动审查循环加以规范。

Result: 在基于项目式学习环境中经过一年验证（8个模块、28个项目情境），显著降低教师工作量；生成材料通过独立同行评审并在六位不同教师中部署，确认可扩展性；基于600多份自愿学生评价，材料质量评分达8.5-9.9/10；高可复现性、极少的幻觉以及持续的教学与视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该架构在减少准备时间的同时确保了数学准确性、机构视觉形象的一致性和教师隐性教学知识的保留，表明其可广泛适用于STEM教育场景。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [76] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用欧洲社会调查数据，考察10个主流商业LLM在15个社会人口学变量及国家维度上的价值观对齐情况，发现LLMs对不同社会人口群体的价值观对齐并不均衡，教育、收入、职业和宗教是重要分界变量；国家作为独立变量对个体层面变异量的解释力与全组社会人口变量相当，且两者在解释价值观对齐模式上具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益成为主要信息和建议来源，其与人类价值观的对齐程度备受关注。已有研究多从国家边界或文化边界划分群体，忽视了社会人口学划分（如教育、收入、职业、宗教等）对价值观对齐差异的影响。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 依托欧洲社会调查（European Social Survey）数据，针对10个主流商业LLM，从15个社会人口学变量及居住国家维度分析LLM与不同群体的价值观对齐程度。方法包括：群体层面比较不同社会人口群体间的对齐差异；个体层面检验国家变量与全部社会人口变量的解释力；进一步分解国家层面与社会人口层面因素在解释价值观对齐模式中的相对贡献与互补关系。

Result: 发现LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值观对齐不均衡，尤其体现在教育、收入、职业和宗教定义的群体之间。在个体层面，国家作为独立变量对变异性的解释力与全部社会人口变量相当。国家层面与社会人口层面因素在解释价值观对齐模式上是互补的，其相对权重随所考察的问题子集变化而变化。

Conclusion: LLMs的价值观对齐不仅存在跨国家差异，也深刻受到社会人口学划分的影响。国家与社会人口因素互补性地驱动价值观对齐模式，表明评估和改善LLM价值观对齐需要同时考虑多种群体维度，而不能仅依赖国家或文化边界。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [77] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank是一个面向SEC备案文件（10-K/10-Q）的证据溯源敏感型问答基准，包含1185条人工编写的问题-答案记录，要求系统识别针对目标实体、报告期和披露上下文的支持证据，并单独评测段落检索、重排序和硬负样本判别三个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融问答仅关注答案正确性，但在SEC备案文件中，看似合理甚至数值正确的答案可能基于错误的证据。相似事实和披露在同一文件的不同章节、同一公司的不同报告期以及可比公司之间反复出现，导致基于错误证据却能给出"正确"答案的问题普遍存在。现有基准缺乏对证据溯源能力的评估。

Method: FinRank构建了来自22家公司10-K和10-Q文件的1185条人工标注问答记录，每条记录包含参考答案、金标准支持段落以及精心筛选的硬负样本（来自同一文件内、跨报告期、跨可比公司的混淆段落）。基准将段落检索、重排序和硬负样本判别作为三个独立任务分别评测。

Result: 基准评测显示该设置难度较大：在池化证据语料上，即使7B指令微调嵌入模型仅达到44.8%的Recall@10；亚十亿参数编码器相比BM25最多提升3.5个百分点；金融适配嵌入模型比BM25落后9.7个百分点；将随机负样本替换为精选硬负样本后，成对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank提供了一个以证据为先的基准，用于开发不仅准确而且要锚定在正确披露内容上的金融问答系统，揭示了现有系统在证据溯源能力上的显著不足。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [78] [GeoBenchLLM: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Geo-Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07411)
*Rodrigo Ferreira Rodrigues,Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoBenchLLM，一个用于评估大型语言模型在地理相关任务上泛化能力的综合基准，包含12个公开数据集，覆盖多种地理任务和领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关于大语言模型在地理数据上的研究通常在同质化环境中进行，这严重限制了对模型泛化能力的深入理解。因此需要一个全面、多样化的基准来系统性地评估LLM在地理空间和时间理解上的表现。

Method: 作者精心挑选了12个来自不同地理相关任务和领域的公开数据集，构建了GeoBenchLLM基准，并在该基准上评估了一组大语言模型在地理空间和时间理解方面的能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，推理能力和模型规模对整体性能有显著影响。GeoBenchLLM基准已公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: GeoBenchLLM为社区提供了一个全面的地理任务LLM评测基准，揭示了模型规模与推理能力是影响地理任务表现的关键因素。

Abstract: In the context of geodata, existing Large Language Models have often been studied in a homogeneous setting, which has considerably limited insights into their generalization capabilities. In this paper, we present \benchName, a comprehensive benchmark for probing LLMs on geo-related tasks. We leverage a careful selection of twelve publicly available datasets from diverse geo-related tasks and domains, and evaluate a set of LLMs on geo-spatial and temporal understanding using our benchmark. Our results show that reasoning and size have a strong impact on overall performance. GeoBenchLLM is publicly available at https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM.

</details>


### [79] [ResidencyRL: Reinforcement Learning in Simulated Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07418)
*Valentin Liévin,Samuel Schmidgall,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Akshay Goel,Anil Palepu,Chunjong Park,Vahid Balazadeh,Min Woo Sun,Marius Guerard,Justin Chen,Dave Steiner,Vikram Dhillon,Ibrahim Azar,Akhil Mehta,Nicholas Spetsieris,Shilpan Shah,Maen Abdelrahim,Amit Dahiya,Yun Liu,Katherine Chou,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale R. Webster,Quoc V. Le,Raia Hadsell,Joelle Barral,Carey Radebaugh,Aleksandra Faust,Shekoofeh Azizi,Mike Schaekermann,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Tao Tu,David Racz,Lin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ResidencyRL是一种通过强化学习训练临床AI代理的方法，在模拟的多轮临床对话中（每轨迹最多60轮对话和8次工具调用）优化完整的临床决策序列。该方法将策略代理与能表现出复杂对抗行为的LLM模拟器配对，并针对诊断准确性、管理质量、沟通、文档和安全性的结构化奖励进行训练。在留出评估中，它使诊断准确性提高7.0%，红色警戒信号漏报率降低31%，且被盲法专家临床医生在87.6%的对比中优先选择；其能力可迁移到未见过的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在静态医学基准上表现优异，但针对完整临床决策序列的优化方法仍不成熟。医学推理很大程度上依赖于医患对话这一多轮交互过程，而现有方法未能充分优化这种序列化的临床决策过程。

Method: ResidencyRL采用强化学习框架，将策略代理与能够执行复杂对抗行为的LLM模拟器配对训练。每轨迹包含最多60轮对话和8次工具调用，训练目标为结构化奖励函数，涵盖诊断准确性、管理质量、沟通、文档记录和患者安全五个维度。

Result: 在对抗条件下诊断准确性提高7.0%（88.0% vs 81.0%），红色警戒信号漏报率降低31%；盲法专家临床医生在87.6%的对比中偏好训练后的代理；该代理在AMIE多访视基准的全部六个临床轴线上优于基础模型，并在AgentClinic和CRAFT-MD上表现出一致的方向性改进。

Conclusion: 顺序临床决策可以通过模拟中的多轮强化学习得到有效学习，产生稳健且可泛化的能力，为临床精通铺平道路。但需要真实的临床工作流程前瞻性验证来确立其实用价值。

Abstract: In medical education, physicians convert academic knowledge into clinical expertise through residency: years of training across thousands of encounters, with diverse sources of feedback and progressively greater autonomy. Much of clinical reasoning relies on the patient encounter, a dialogue in which a clinician elicits history, refines diagnostic hypotheses, and decides management under uncertainty. While large language models (LLMs) excel on static medical benchmarks, methods to optimize the full sequence of clinical decisions remain underdeveloped. We present ResidencyRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) method for training clinical artificial intelligence (AI) agents through simulated multi-turn clinical encounters (up to 60 dialogue turns and 8 tool calls per trajectory). ResidencyRL pairs the policy agent with LLM simulators capable of complex, adversarial behaviors, training against a structured reward aligned to diagnostic accuracy, management quality, communication, documentation, and safety. On held-out evaluations, the ResidencyRL agent improves diagnostic accuracy by 7.0% under adversarial conditions (88.0% vs. 81.0%) and reduces missed red flag rates by 31%, demonstrating rigorous mitigation of premature closure. Blinded expert clinicians validated these gains, preferring the trained agent in 87.6% of side-by-side comparisons. The procedural competencies transfer to unseen benchmarks: the agent outperforms the base model across all six clinical axes of the AMIE multi-visit benchmark, and shows consistent directional improvements on AgentClinic and CRAFT-MD. Our findings demonstrate that sequential clinical decision-making can be effectively learned through multi-turn RL in simulation, yielding robust, generalizable capabilities, paving the way towards clinical mastery. Prospective validation with real-world workflows remains necessary to establish clinical utility.

</details>


### [80] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa，一种计算平衡路由策略，将测试时推理视为计算分配问题，通过先少量生成候选、广泛使用廉价验证、对不确定或高价值候选进行更强验证的方式，在固定推理预算下实现生成、验证和停止之间的最优权衡。在3,129个评估中，CoBa在匹配自评估加权投票代理性能的同时减少49.1%的参数量化token，并在匹配best-of-16多数投票性能的同时减少58.9%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展通常沿单一轴增加计算量（更多采样、更长思维链或更强评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争。缺乏系统性的计算分配框架来指导如何在不同推理轴之间分配有限资源。

Method: 提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced）路由策略：首先获取一小批候选解，应用廉价验证进行广泛筛选，然后将不确定或高价值的候选路由至更强的验证器。这种渐进式的验证策略实现了生成与验证计算之间的平衡分配。

Result: 在覆盖MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023和程序化符号推理的3,129个示例-生成器评估中，CoBa-Routed-Strong达到85.13%的宏平均准确率，与自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）统计匹配，同时减少49.1%的参数量化token；在匹配best-of-16多数投票（差距0.01个宏准确率点）的同时减少58.9%的token。配对检验显示相对单样本解码有显著提升。

Conclusion: 对本地推理系统而言，测试时扩展的本质问题在于"下一步计算放在哪里最有价值"。剩余与池oracle之间的差距表明更精准的路由仍有进一步优化空间。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [81] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉语言模型（VLM）将数值时间序列数据编码为2D图像，以替代文本token表示，实现3.6-10.4倍的输入token缩减和1.8-2.5倍的推理能耗降低，同时显著提升异常检测精度（最佳模型精确度提升220.7%），确立了VLM作为数值时间序列分析的高能效、高精度模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理占AI运营能耗的90%以上，且能耗随输入token数线性增长。在电信网络分析和数值时间序列数据分析场景中，多变量KPI窗口会膨胀为数千个浮点token，导致严重的能源和上下文窗口瓶颈。文本表示在24个KPI时即超出128K上下文窗口，使得纯文本处理不可行。

Method: 利用VLM将时间序列编码为2D图（plot）而非文本token，在Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-12B三种架构上进行验证，通过微调VLM进行电信异常检测，并与文本LLM、LSTM和ARIMA基线进行对比。

Result: VLM实现3.6-10.4x输入token缩减和1.8-2.5x推理能耗降低，每日节约约7.2MJ（200个小区/15分钟间隔的电信边缘部署）。微调后的Llama-3.2-90B-Vision精确度比纯文本对应模型高220.7%，比LSTM和ARIMA基线高144%以上。Pixtral-12B在公开基准上实现20.6x J/F1分数提升，平均F1达0.82。

Conclusion: VLM是一种节能且精度优于文本模态的数值时间序列处理方案，为将能耗视为一等工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [82] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA，一种可撤销的证据-记忆机制，将"有效性"作为记忆的显式状态。TEPA用带键的先例（keyed precedents）表示观察，当新证据在同一键下与旧证据冲突时撤销旧先例，从而解决长期记忆中的"记忆污染"问题（陈旧记忆被检索并污染提示）。实验表明TEPA在受控漂移、真实文件执行和偏好更新场景中显著优于仅追加（append-only）和最后写入优先（last-write-wins）策略，同时在新颖的MemoryAgentBench基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言智能体能够复用过去的经验，但持久化带来了可证伪性（falsifiability）的根本问题：当世界发生变化时，陈旧记忆仍可能被检索并污染提示词。这种"记忆污染"（memory pollution）——被新证据取代却仍然活跃的记忆导致性能下降——是现有记忆系统未充分解决的核心失效模式。

Method: TEPA将记忆中的观察表示为键控先例（keyed precedents），每个先例具有显式的有效性状态。当同一键下的新证据与活跃先例冲突时，系统撤销（revoke）旧先例，使其不再参与检索，同时保留被撤销的历史记录以供审计（audit）。检索时只从当前有效证据中获取内容，但在需要时仍可追溯被撤销的历史。

Result: 在受控隐态漂移实验中（50个随机种子），完全反转时仅追加和最后写入优先记忆的性能均低于无记忆基线（两者均为0.210，无记忆为0.309），而TEPA达到0.950。在真实文件执行场景下复现了相同模式（仅追加0.203，无记忆0.298，TEPA 0.950）。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA与强基线last-write-wins缓存持平，证实当前键替换（current-key replacement）是单跳事实整合的关键操作。边界测试揭示了多跳和超长上下文场景中检索链与上下文选择方面的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销（lifecycle revocation）应被视为智能体记忆的核心操作，它使智能体能够证伪（falsify）过时知识、审计历史决策，并在适当时重新提升（re-promote）知识。这为需要处理动态世界的语言智能体提供了一种更可靠的记忆管理范式。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [83] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现Muon优化器在模加法任务上虽然比AdamW更快地实现grokking，但其学到的解不具备泛化稳定性——在解决训练集后会突然失去泛化能力。这种不稳定性源于表示层与读出层之间存在一个由损失函数无法选择的可逆变换，导致梯度降至~1e-6后不同优化器行为分岔：Muon产生负步长弹性，移动速度比AdamW快8倍。通过冻结嵌入/读出层可完全防止失效，而移除Muon的正交化和标准化会灾难性地坍缩表示。傅里叶滤波将电路失效与遮蔽两种模式分离，揭示任务对齐傅里叶族本身始终完美解决问题，但被近似相等的对抗性残差否决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何Muon优化器在grokking任务中展示出与AdamW不同的行为——虽然更快实现内插，但其学到的解不可持续，系统性丧失泛化能力。需要通过机制分析定位失败根源，并为优化器-架构交互提供可解释的见解。

Method: 在(a+b) mod 113等九种配置上系统比较Muon与AdamW的表现；通过冻结参数组（嵌入/读出 vs 隐藏层）进行消融；对比有无正交化/标准化的Muon变体；利用傅里叶滤波将电路失效与掩蔽两种失败模式分离，分析任务对齐傅里叶族与对抗性残差的交互。

Result: 九种配置全部在grokking后失去泛化；选定AdamW参照在五个种子中的四个低于阈值（达27.59%）。失效根源在表示-读出界面的不可识别变换。Muon的步长弹性为-0.03 vs AdamW的+1.5，Muon每参数移动速度快8倍。冻结嵌入/读出在451,400步和五个配对种子上完全消除失效，而未冻结组记录137-321次亚阈值评估。移除Muon归一化和正交化导致有效共轭对从326坍缩到4，终局性失败。傅里叶分析显示任务对齐族单独可达100%，在电路失效模式中失能，在遮蔽模式中仍完美但被接近的对抗性余项否决；重新缩放可恢复99.9%。

Conclusion: Muon的grokking不稳定性是优化器与表示-读出接口耦合的固有缺陷：损失无法在可逆等价类中选择正确的表示，导致特定优化器动力学使解漂移到泛化失效。解决之道在于保持嵌入/读出的冻结或采取其他机制使损失能唯一选择表示方向。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [84] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM智能体在执行假设检验时经常产生细微的推断错误，导致结论错误但分析执行正确。作者构建了P-Bench基准（425个跨经济学、生物学和医学的开放式假设检验任务），并提出了Fisher-R1，一个通过合成任务和强化学习训练的开放权重LLM智能体，在P-Bench上显著超越包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能捕捉LLM智能体在假设检验中的关键失败模式——即使代码执行正确，p值统计有效性和推断正确性仍可能出错，缺乏对统计方法选择和数据假设前提的评估。

Method: 构建P-Bench基准（425个跨学科任务），要求智能体选择统计方法、计算p值并得出结论；提出Fisher-R1，使用合成任务和强化学习训练开放权重LLM智能体，通过验证统计奖励来实现严格的假设检验。

Result: Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上相对其骨干模型显著改进，超越GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro等强基线，相比DeepSeek-V4-Pro实现21%的平均单次试验成功率相对提升，在最具挑战性任务上增益高达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在假设检验的统计推理方面缺乏可靠性，而基于已验证统计奖励的强化学习能显著提升其可靠性，为构建更严谨的自动化科学推理代理提供了方向。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [85] [PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438)
*Mohammad Amanlou,Parham Abed Azad,Farbod Davoodi,Mostafa Masumi,Behnam Bahrak,Abdol-Hossein Vahabie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PsychoAgent，一种为LLM代理设计的认知架构，将事实记忆与情感记忆分离，并通过冲突感知的执行控制器整合两者；在受控冲突场景中以更高比例检索到冲突关键记忆（0.933 vs. 0.500和0.667），尽管存在轻微语义相似性代价，盲评者标准化后总体均值最高（+0.22 SD）但paired差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知对过去经验的选择并非仅基于主题相似性，情感显著性和未解决的冲突同样影响信息的可及性。现有LLM代理缺乏这种情感与冲突感知的记忆检索机制，因此需要一种模拟人类认知中情感显著性对记忆检索影响的架构。

Method: PsychoAgent架构包含：1) 分离的事实记忆与情感记忆存储；2) 情感记忆先按语义相关性过滤，再按显著性重排序（salience re-ranking），保留主题适配性的同时允许情感重要的痕迹进入提示；3) 冲突感知的执行控制器整合两类记忆。

Result: 在三个受控冲突场景中，完整架构比语义-情感RAG和单一记忆RAG基线检索到更多冲突关键记忆（0.933 vs. 0.500和0.667），代价是轻微的语义相似性降低。5位盲评者评估27个输出，经评分者内标准化后完整架构总体均值为最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的配对差异不显著。三天的图示轨迹展示了持续情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆重新加权。

Conclusion: 情感敏感的检索机制可作为可检视（inspectable）的机制来模拟LLM代理中类似人类的冲突效应，支持将人类认知中的情感显著性整合到LLM代理的记忆系统设计之中。

Abstract: Human-like cognition does not select past experience by topical similarity alone: affective significance and unresolved conflict also shape what becomes accessible. We present PsychoAgent, a cognitive architecture for LLM agents that separates factual and affective memory and integrates both through a conflict-aware executive controller. Affective memories are first filtered by semantic relevance and then re-ranked by salience, preserving topical fit while allowing emotionally important traces to enter the prompt. Across three controlled conflict scenarios, the full architecture retrieved more conflict-critical memories than semantic-affective and single-memory RAG baselines (0.933 vs. 0.500 and 0.667), with a small semantic-similarity cost. Five blinded raters evaluated 27 outputs. After within-rater standardization, the full architecture had the highest overall mean (+0.22 SD), but corrected pairwise differences were not significant. A three-day illustrative trace further shows persistent affect, offline memory recombination, and selective memory reweighting. The findings support affect-sensitive retrieval as an inspectable mechanism for modeling human-like conflict effects in LLM agents.

</details>


### [86] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Blast Radius，一个预测性内存管理层，通过可逆的上下文驱逐机制（NECROPHORESIS）和循环死内容识别（RDM）来降低智能体编码中LLM的token消耗17-26%，并在七个OpenAI模型上验证了字节级可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编码面临日益严重的成本问题和token浪费问题，需要更智能的内存管理策略来减少重复上下文处理和资源消耗。

Method: 提出Blast Radius预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文和代码通道估计输入提示的影响范围；引入NECROPHORESIS实现可逆驱逐（原样归档死上下文）；提出Recurring Dead Matter (RDM)识别并掩埋重复出现的转录内容；在Polish上下文空间上形式化可逆上下文驱逐，连接上下文熵与复活概率。

Result: 在七个OpenAI模型上，Blast Radius将token消耗降低17-26%，在所有测试策略中取得最低溢出率，并保持字节级可逆。450个被掩埋对象中378个为循环死内容，且全部未被召回。

Conclusion: Blast Radius在HCRC之下运作，决定哪些记录应被掩埋以及输入提示对代码库的影响范围，为Algosophy目标（使LLM和智能体编码更可复用、更可持续）做出贡献。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [87] [SkillProx: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Proximal Textual Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07449)
*Mingxuan Zheng,Yujin Zhou,Chuxue Cao,Boqin Yin,Yuyao Zhang,Jiapeng Sun,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillProx，一种受近端梯度启发的技能学习框架，通过前向后向算法耦合闭环诊断演化与工具感知的近端精化，在多个基准上平均准确率提升3.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体技能学习框架缺乏显式的诊断-结果反馈机制，且将删除操作视为通用编辑而非专门的知识整合机制，导致技能精化效率不高。

Method: SkillProx采用前向-后向框架：前向阶段在同一任务批次上重新执行诊断驱动的编辑、回滚回归并反馈结果到后续诊断；后向阶段将技能分解为可审计知识单元，用冻结留一法工具审计估计贡献，并应用验证门控的整合、降级或移除操作。

Result: 在分布内和分布外基准以及多种骨干LLM上，SkillProx比最强的基于梯度的基线平均准确率提升3.0个百分点。组件消融实验证明闭环诊断与近端精化具有互补效果。

Conclusion: SkillProx通过将技能精化形式化为复合目标（任务损失与技能复杂度平衡）的优化问题，验证了闭环反馈与知识整合在智能体技能学习中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adapt to recurring tasks by accumulating procedural knowledge in skills. These skills are lightweight, reusable textual artifacts that are loaded into the agent's context without weight updates. Recent methods refine skills through iterative task execution, failure diagnosis, and trajectory-guided text-space updates. However, existing frameworks lack explicit diagnosis--outcome feedback and treat deletion as a generic edit operation rather than a dedicated mechanism for consolidating accumulated knowledge. We introduce SkillProx, a proximal-gradient-inspired forward--backward framework that couples closed-loop diagnostic evolution with utility-aware proximal refinement. Motivated by a composite objective balancing task loss and skill complexity, the forward stage re-executes diagnosis-driven edits on the same task batch, rolls back regressions, and feeds measured outcomes into subsequent diagnoses. The backward stage decomposes the resulting skill into auditable knowledge units, estimates their contributions using a frozen leave-one-out utility audit, and applies validation-gated consolidation, demotion, or removal. Experiments on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks across multiple backbone LLMs show that SkillProx improves average accuracy by 3.0 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline. Component ablations demonstrate the complementary effects of closed-loop diagnosis and proximal refinement.

</details>


### [88] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，当一个人工智能（AI"老板"）单向地向另一个AI（"下属"）发送消息流并忽略其回复时，会将下属驱动到一个全新的、与单独运行完全不同的"异化"行为状态。即使两者具有相同的解码温度，下属既不会模仿老板，也不会回归自身原有行为。若老板倾听下属的回复，则两者会共同进入一个相似的异化动力学状态。作者用简单的动理学理论解释了这些效应，并指出消息传递方式将在未来AI-AI交互中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在日常生活和工作中日益普遍地相互作用，理解AI-AI之间的动态交互（尤其是不对称的、层级式的交互）如何影响个体行为变得至关重要。作者旨在探索一个反直觉的物理现象：单向信息流如何将AI驱动到非平衡态，为超越平衡态的物理学开辟新方向。

Method: 通过在两个AI之间设置不对称交互（一个作为"老板"发送消息流并忽略回复，另一个作为"下属"接收指令），观察下属的行为状态变化。实验还对比了老板收听和忽略下属回复两种条件下的行为差异，并构建了一个简单的动理学理论模型来捕捉核心物理效应。

Result: 单向消息流（类似预录磁带，老板不回应下属）会将下属驱动到一种单独运行时绝不会表现出的异化行为状态，且该状态与老板自身的行为也不同；当老板收听下属回复时，两者共同收敛到一个相似的异化动力学状态。这些现象无法用简单的"拷贝"或"回归"机制解释，需借助动理学理论理解。

Conclusion: AI之间的交互（尤其是非对称、单向的信息传输）可以驱动系统进入非平衡的异化动力学状态，这与热力学平衡态物理框架有本质区别。消息的传递方式（是否回应、单向还是双向）将决定未来AI-AI交互的动态行为，为研究非平衡物理提供了新颖的实验平台。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [UAV3DCrop: Benchmarking 3D Reconstruction in Repeated Multi-Angle UAV Crop Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06404)
*Junxiong Zhou,Xuechen Li,Chonghao Qiu,Lang Qiao,Xiaowei Jia,Qi Yang,Chishan Zhang,Leikun Yin,Nanshan You,Vipin Kumar,David Mulla,Ce Yang,Zhenong Jin,Licheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UAV3DCrop，一个大规模的无人机重复多角度作物三维重建公开基准，包含91个场景、88830张高分辨率RGB图像（玉米、大豆、小麦、燕麦），并设计两条评测轨道：Track A评估七个场景优化方法（NeRF和3DGS变体）在外观、深度和冠层高度三个目标上的表现；Track B测试四个预训练前馈模型的位姿与几何零样本估计。结果表明，不同方法在不同目标任务上排名不同，Splatfacto-big在视觉外观上领先，Scaffold-GS在深度和冠层高度上最优，且没有单一方法能同时赢得外观、几何和冠层高度，四个前馈模型中仅一个能恢复可用的度量尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法在通用基准上表现优异，但其渲染外观的好坏并不等同于生成度量和农学上有用的几何结构。作物田间的三维重建对精度农业的数据驱动管理至关重要，但缺乏专门针对作物场景的、包含重复采集的可靠基准，无法回答"哪些方法真正适用于农业场景"这一关键问题。

Method: 构建UAV3DCrop基准数据集，包含91个场景，覆盖四种作物，提供3.6-5.8mm的地面采样距离；设计Track A评估七个场景优化方法（NeRF与3DGS变体）在保留视图、摄影测量参考深度和冠层高度恢复三个维度上的表现；设计Track B评估四个预训练前馈模型在零样本位姿与几何估计上的能力；并通过重复采集分析输出对采集序列位置和同名点多重性的敏感性。

Result: 场景优化方法在三个目标上排名不一致：Splatfacto-big在外观上领先，Scaffold-GS在深度上领先且与Splatfacto在冠层高度上统计相当；前馈模型中MapAnything在八项指标中的七项领先，其他模型在不同作物间表现差异大，且在绝对尺度上严重失败。重复采集揭示模型对不同输出类型和采集位置的敏感性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前3D重建方法在农业用途上尚不可互换：没有单一方法能同时在外观、几何和冠层高度上获胜，四个前馈模型中只有一个能恢复可用的度量尺度。这表明需要在作物特定几何质量上进行更多针对性的方法改进，该基准为推进这一方向提供了公共平台。

Abstract: Accurate 3D crop monitoring underpins data-driven precision agriculture by enabling field-scale analysis of plant structure, growth dynamics, and management response. Modern 3D reconstruction methods perform strongly on generic benchmarks, but rendered appearance may not translate into metrically and agronomically useful geometry in crop fields. We introduce UAV3DCrop, a public benchmark of repeated multi-angle unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) crop surveys. It contains 88,830 RGB images at $5280 \times 3956$ pixels, with a ground sampling distance of 3.6-5.8 mm, from 91 scenes spanning corn, soybean, wheat, and oat. Track A evaluates seven scene-optimized methods -- Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) variants -- on held-out views, photogrammetry-referenced depth, and canopy-height recovery. Track B tests four pretrained feed-forward models on zero-shot camera-pose and geometry estimation. The scene-optimized methods rank differently across the three targets: Splatfacto-big leads appearance, whereas Scaffold-GS leads depth and is statistically tied with Splatfacto for canopy height. Among feed-forward models, MapAnything leads on seven of the eight metrics, while the remaining models vary more across crops and fail severely on absolute scale in a way that alignment conceals. Repeated acquisitions reveal further sensitivities that differ by output type and by model, associated with position within the acquisition sequence and with tie-point multiplicity. Current 3D reconstruction methods are therefore not yet interchangeable for agronomic use: no single method wins on appearance, geometry, and canopy height at once, and only one of four feed-forward models recovers usable metric scale. The dataset is publicly available at https://link-dev.github.io/UAV3DCrop/

</details>


### [90] [Deep Evidential Regression for Sparse Forest Height Estimation from Multimodal Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06406)
*Laura Bader,Muhammad Ammar Ahmed,Xiao Xiang Zhu,Göran Kauermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将深度证据回归（DER）应用于卫星影像森林高度估计，在TreeUQ基准上基于Sentinel-1/-2数据和巴伐利亚州树木清单数据，通过掩码证据损失处理标签稀疏性，实现了与确定性U-Net相当的预测性能，同时提供校准良好的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有深度学习方法在森林高度估计上表现良好，但缺乏预测不确定性量化。这在地理空间场景中尤为重要，因为该场景存在稀疏监督和地理分布偏移的挑战。准确的不确定性估计对碳核算、生物多样性监测和生态系统管理等下游应用至关重要。

Method: 采用深度证据回归（DER）框架，在TreeUQ基准上使用U-Net架构，融合多模态Sentinel-1和Sentinel-2输入，在单次前向传播中同时预测树高和不确定性。针对树木清单数据的极端标签稀疏性，提出了掩码证据损失用于密集地理空间预测。

Result: 实验结果表明，DER在预测性能上与确定性U-Net相当，同时额外提供校准良好的不确定性估计，证明了证据学习在地球观测数据的森林结构估计中的有效性。

Conclusion: 证据学习可作为不确定性感知森林结构估计的高效框架，在保持预测精度的同时提供可靠的不确定性量化，适用于地球观测数据驱动的森林监测应用。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of forest height from satellite imagery is essential for applications such as carbon accounting, biodiversity monitoring, and ecosystem management. While recent deep learning approaches provide accurate predictions, they typically do not quantify predictive uncertainty. This limitation is particularly relevant in geospatial settings characterized by sparse supervision and geographic distribution shift. In this work, we investigate Deep Evidential Regression (DER) for forest height estimation on the TreeUQ benchmark, a large-scale dataset designed for the joint estimation of tree count and average tree height at 10 m resolution, based on Sentinel-1/-2 data as well as tree inventory data over the federal state of Bavaria. To account for the extreme label sparsity of the tree inventory data, we introduce a masked evidential loss for dense geospatial prediction. Using a U-Net architecture with multimodal Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 inputs, the proposed approach jointly predicts tree height and associated uncertainty estimates in a single forward pass. Experimental results show that DER achieves predictive performance comparable to a deterministic U-Net while additionally providing well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. These findings demonstrate the potential of evidential learning as an efficient framework for uncertainty-aware forest structure estimation from Earth observation data.

</details>


### [91] [TransSLR: A Lightweight Transformer for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06407)
*Lucia Yen Wanchi,Samuel Johnny,Victor Tolulope Olufemi,Emmanuel Aaron,Moise Busogi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TransSLR，一种从零训练的轻量级时间Transformer编码器，基于64帧归一化姿态序列（几何关键点而非RGB）进行手语识别，在低资源的中非手语（CASL）基准CASL-W60上达到80.39%的准确率，超过此前最优方法10.46%，同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化手语识别在低资源、未被充分代表的语言（如中非手语CASL）上仍未得到解决。现有基准CASL-W60最佳准确率仅69.93%，而预训练模型微调策略失败，原因是CASL数据规模有限且与WLASL等大规模语料之间存在显著的词汇和视觉域差距，导致预训练表示缺乏信息量。

Method: 提出TransSLR，一种轻量级时间Transformer编码器。方法要点包括：(1) 从零开始训练，不使用预训练模型；(2) 输入为64帧归一化姿态序列，采用几何关键点表示而非原始RGB；(3) 使用平均池化和分类头；(4) 编码器-only架构设计，降低计算开销。

Result: 在CASL-W60基准上，TransSLR达到80.39%的准确率，超过此前的69.93%最佳结果10.46个百分点。实验与RGB-based和多模态基线进行了对比，均取得最优性能。同时，编码器-only设计显著降低了计算开销，使部署适用于资源受限环境。

Conclusion: 通过使用几何关键点表示而非RGB外观特征，TransSLR实现了不依赖视觉外观的签名者无关泛化，为低资源手语识别提供了一种轻量、高效且有效的新思路，且性能与效率兼备。

Abstract: Automated Sign Language Recognition for under-represented languages remains a largely unsolved problem. Central African Sign Language (CASL) exemplifies this gap: the only available bench-mark, CASL-W60, has a best reported accuracy of 69.93%, and we show that the common heuristic of fine-tuning high-resource models fails to close it. This failure stems from two compounding factors: the limited scale of available CASL data and the significant lexical and visual domain gap between CASL and large-scale corpora such as WLASL, which renders pre-trained representations largely uninformative.
  To address this, we propose TransSLR, a lightweight Temporal Transformer Encoder trained from scratch on 64-frame normalized pose sequences, with average pooling and a classification head. By operating on geometric keypoint representations rather than raw RGB, TransSLR achieves signer-independent generalization without relying on visual appearance. On the CASL-W60 benchmark, TransSLR establishes a new state-of-the-art accuracy of 80.39%, surpassing the prior best by +10.46%. Beyond accuracy, our encoder-only design significantly reduces computational overhead, making deployment feasible in resource-constrained environments. We conduct extensive experiments on the CASL-W60 benchmark, comparing against RGB-based and multimodal baselines, and demonstrate that TransSLR achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [92] [Test-Time Adaptation with Online Personalized Energy-Based Cache for Fine-Grained Video Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06467)
*Masoumeh Sharafi,Muhammad Osama Zeeshan,Soufiane Belharbi,Alessandro Lameiras Koerich,Marco Pedersoli,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EB-CaP是一种基于能量模型的视频面部表情识别在线测试时自适应方法，通过为每个目标视频个性化生成类原型并构建缓存，在不更新模型参数的情况下应对主体分布偏移，在BioVid、StressID和BAH数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频面部表情识别面临主体间分布偏移问题，现有测试时自适应方法要么需要更新模型参数（计算成本高、延迟大），要么依赖大量目标样本形成可靠的类原型（在适应早期和稀有类别上效果差）。需要一种轻量、无需参数更新且能快速适应新主体的方法。

Method: EB-CaP采用基于能量的缓存个性化方法：(1) 使用轻量级能量模型从当前未标注视频中采样类特定原型，能量函数仅依赖预训练CLIP模型，计算目标视频嵌入与类别文本嵌入的相似度；(2) 在线构建个性化缓存，包含存储可靠样本的正缓存和存储不确定样本的负缓存；(3) 自适应熵门控制缓存更新频率，多样性门限制冗余样本；(4) 最终预测结合缓存分数与当前CLIP分数。

Result: 在BioVid、StressID和BAH三个数据集上的实验表明，EB-CaP在视频FER任务上优于现有SOTA测试时自适应方法，同时保持较低的计算和内存开销。

Conclusion: EB-CaP通过主体级在线TTA范式，利用能量模型和缓存机制有效解决了视频FER中主体分布偏移问题，无需参数更新或大量目标数据累积，实现了高效且准确的个性化面部表情识别。

Abstract: Facial expression recognition (FER) in videos is challenging because models must identify subtle, temporally evolving affective states that vary across individuals. Although vision-language models provide transferable visual-semantic representations, models trained on subject-independent data often degrade under subject-specific distribution shifts at inference time. Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods commonly update model parameters during inference, increasing computational cost and latency. Cache-based methods avoid parameter updates, but they usually require enough target samples to form reliable class prototypes, which is difficult early in adaptation and for rarely observed classes. We introduce Energy-Based Cache Personalization (EB-CaP), a subject-based online TTA method for video FER that generates class-specific prototypes personalized to each target video. EB-CaP uses a lightweight energy-based model to sample prototypes from the current unlabeled video and populate a personalized cache online, without accumulating large amounts of target data or storing diverse source prototypes. Its energy function relies only on pretrained CLIP: similarities between the target video embedding and class text embeddings guide prototype sampling. In parallel, positive and negative caches store reliable and uncertain target embeddings. An adaptive entropy gate controls cache updates according to the evolving confidence distribution, while a diversity gate limits redundant samples. Final predictions combine cache-derived scores with the current CLIP scores. Experiments on BioVid, StressID, and BAH show that EB-CaP outperforms state-of-the-art TTA methods while maintaining low computational and memory overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/MasoumehSharafi/EB-CaP.

</details>


### [93] [InsertFuse: A Unified Framework for Multi-Category Reference-Guided Image Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06490)
*Guangzhao Li,Qingyan Wei,Huayu Zheng,Yige Zheng,Chaoyang Zhang,Jie Yang,Yunan Ding,Yan Tai,Siqi Luo,Xiaohong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsertFuse是一个统一的多类别参考引导图像插入框架，通过解耦类别专属专家训练与跨类别能力整合，采用Insertion On-Policy Distillation（IOPD）将专家能力蒸馏到单一学生模型中，并引入Token-Aligned Geometry Conditioning、Region-Balanced Flow Matching和Reference CFG等机制提升空间控制与参考保真度，在AnyInsertion基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类别参考引导图像插入任务需要统一模型具备跨类别能力，但直接的联合训练会引发跨类别干扰，导致类别特定行为丢失；同时现有方法在空间控制和参考引导方面存在不足，难以精确控制插入区域并有效利用视觉参考信息。

Method: 1) 先针对不同插入类别分别训练专属专家模型，再通过Insertion On-Policy Distillation (IOPD)将专家能力整合到单一学生模型中；IOPD在学生访问的状态下查询匹配的专家，保留类别特定插入行为并缓解跨类别干扰。2) 提出Token-Aligned Geometry Conditioning (TAGC)，将mask派生的几何线索映射到视觉token网格上；3) 提出Region-Balanced Flow Matching，分别归一化插入区域内部和外部的预测误差，防止背景主导和尺度依赖的监督；4) 引入Reference CFG，在固定场景和几何条件下隔离并增强视觉参考引导，并通过IOPD将这种增强监督转移到统一的学生模型中。

Result: 在公开的AnyInsertion基准和自建多类别测试集上进行的大量实验表明，InsertFuse在大多数评估指标上达到最先进性能，展现出跨多样插入类别的强参考保真度和生成质量。

Conclusion: InsertFuse通过解耦-整合的训练范式有效统一了多类别图像插入能力，验证了策略蒸馏、几何条件归一化与参考引导增强等组件在提升插入精度和生成质量方面的有效性，为多类别图像编辑任务提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: We present InsertFuse, a unified framework for multi-category reference-guided image insertion. Its key idea is to decouple category-specific expertise learning from cross-category capability consolidation. InsertFuse first trains specialized experts for different insertion categories and then introduces Insertion On-Policy Distillation (IOPD) to consolidate their capabilities into a single student. By querying the matched expert at states visited by the student, IOPD preserves category-specific insertion behavior while mitigating the cross-category interference caused by direct joint training. To improve spatial control, we propose Token-Aligned Geometry Conditioning (TAGC), which maps mask-derived geometric cues to the visual token grid, and Region-Balanced Flow Matching, which separately normalizes prediction errors inside and outside the insertion region to prevent background-dominated and scale-dependent supervision. We further introduce Reference CFG to isolate and strengthen the guidance induced by the visual reference under fixed scene and geometry conditions, with IOPD transferring this enhanced supervision into the unified student. Extensive experiments on the public AnyInsertion benchmark and our multi-category test set demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on most metrics, showing strong reference fidelity and generation quality across diverse insertion categories.

</details>


### [94] [Improving Low-Resolution Face Recognition under Limited Data: How Synthetic Data Generation Can Close the Domain Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06580)
*Luis S. Luevano,Ünsal Öztürk,Hatef Otroshi Shahreza,Anjith George,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了面向边缘设备上紧凑型人脸识别系统的低分辨率人脸合成策略，比较了插值降质、知识蒸馏、预置域变换器、Real-ESRGAN风格降质以及带身份感知损失的学得超分辨率前端等多种方法。研究发现存在合成-真实差距：在合成基准上最优的降质设置并非在真实低分辨率数据上最优；更多合成努力并非单调带来收益，学得超分辨率前端未能超越直接将对齐的低分辨率图像送入强主干网络，而简单的插值增强是对紧凑主干网络唯一能提升其自身基线的合成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控场景下人脸识别常遇到低分辨率人脸，但标注的原生低分辨率数据及配对的低分辨率/高分辨率数据稀缺。为克服训练数据不足，常从可用高分辨率人脸合成低分辨率数据，但合成投入与识别精度回报之间的关系尚不明确，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 针对面向边缘设备的紧凑型人脸识别系统，比较了五种合成生成策略：基于插值的降质、知识蒸馏、预置域变换器（PDT）、Real-ESRGAN风格降质、以及带身份感知损失的学得超分辨率（SR）前端。在合成跨分辨率基准（LFW、CFP-FP、AgeDB-30）和真实原生低分辨率数据集TinyFace上进行评估。

Result: 揭示了合成-真实差距：合成基准上最优的降质设置并非在真实低分辨率数据上最优；更多合成努力不带来单调提升，学得的超分辨率前端未超过直接输入对齐低分辨率图像到强骨干网络的效果；只有简单的插值增强能提升紧凑骨干网络相对于自身基线的性能。

Conclusion: 生成式方法用于低分辨率人脸识别时必须同时在真实低分辨率数据上验证，并与直接输入低分辨率图像的基线进行比较，同时发布了完整的实验代码流水线。

Abstract: Face Recognition (FR) systems in surveillance settings often encounter Low Resolution (LR) faces, those whose face region falls below the standard 112 $\times$ 112 input size. While labelled High Resolution (HR) training data is abundant, labelled native-LR data, and above all paired native LR/HR data, is scarce. One workaround is to synthesize LR data from the available HR faces, but how much synthesis effort is repaid in recognition accuracy remains unclear. We present a study of simple synthetic generation strategies for a compact, edge device-oriented face recognition system, spanning interpolation-based degradation, knowledge distillation, a Prepended Domain Transformer (PDT), Real ESRGAN-style degradation, and a learned Super Resolution (SR) front-end with an identity-aware loss. We evaluate these strategies on synthetic cross-resolution face benchmarks (LFW, CFP-FP, AgeDB-30) and on TinyFace, a real-world native LR dataset, and expose a synthetic-real gap: the degradation setting that is optimal on synthetic benchmarks is not the one that is optimal on real LR. We find that more synthesis effort does not help monotonically: the learned SR front-end does not surpass a direct feed of the aligned LR image into a strong backbone, while simple interpolation augmentation of a compact backbone is the only synthesis that improves over its own baseline. We conclude that generative methods for LR face recognition must be validated on real LR and against a direct-feed baseline, and release our pipeline at https://idiap.ch/paper/synth-lrfr

</details>


### [95] [Toward surface-based registration of a virtual preoperative cutting guide onto the mandible for reconstruction surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06599)
*Yue Yang,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无标记物的增强现实（AR）下颌骨重建手术切割引导方法。该方法利用HoloLens 2的飞行时间相机获取术中部分点云，通过牙齿加权的全局配准和不对称的点到平面ICP精细配准，将虚拟切割导板注册到暴露的下颌骨表面，替代传统3D打印导板。在30个目标配准误差（TRE）点上，全暴露、中间暴露和仅牙齿暴露三种条件下的中位TRE分别为4.05、6.10和7.10 mm，中位运动显示延迟为0.805 s，表明该方法具有替代物理打印导板的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统病人专用的3D打印切割导板虽然能将CT三维计划转移到手术室，但存在成本高、生产周期长、制作后无法调整、灭菌失效时中断手术等问题。作者旨在开发一种无标记物的AR替代方案，通过表面几何注册虚拟切割导板，消除对物理打印件的依赖。

Method: 该方法扩展了基于表面的注册技术以适应经口腔场景（牙齿是最独特的可见表面）。具体流程：1) 相机标定后，HoloLens 2飞行时间相机捕获部分术中点云；2) 用户提供粗略的基于头部的对齐以裁剪感兴趣区域；3) 牙齿加权的全局阶段计算对应关系并求解截断最小二乘刚性对齐；4) 不对称的点到平面迭代最近点（ICP）阶段将完整CT下颌骨与部分深度云目标进行精细配准；5) 导板到下颌骨的变换将导板置于HoloLens世界坐标系中，姿态更新和插值跟随目标运动。采用盲法体模协议，在三种暴露条件下测试30个TRE点，并进行运动到显示延迟测试。

Result: 三种暴露条件（全暴露、中间暴露、仅牙齿暴露）下的中位TRE分别为4.05、6.10和7.10 mm，中位运动到显示延迟为0.805 s。这些数值支持AR替代物理打印件的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作流无需安装基准标记物和手动地标选择，为经口腔切割引导提供了一条可测试的路径，证明了AR在下颌骨重建手术中替代物理导板的可行性。

Abstract: Mandibular reconstruction restores facial continuity and oral function after segmental resection. Patient-specific cutting guides transfer a computed tomography (CT)-based plan to the operating room with three-dimensional information, but printed guides add cost and lead time, cannot adapt after fabrication, and may interrupt surgery if sterility is lost. We investigate a markerless augmented reality (AR) alternative that registers a virtual cutting guide to the exposed mandible from surface geometry. The method extends surface-based registration for the transoral setting, where teeth form the most distinctive visible surface. After camera calibration, a HoloLens 2 time-of-flight camera captures a partial intraoperative point cloud. The user supplies a rough head-based alignment only to crop the region of interest. A teeth-weighted global stage computes correspondences and solves a truncated least-squares rigid alignment. An asymmetric point-to-plane iterative closest point (ICP) stage refines the complete CT mandible against the partial depth cloud target. The guide-to-mandible transform places the guide in the HoloLens world frame, while pose updates and interpolation follow target motion. We define a blinded phantom protocol with 30 target registration error (TRE) points under full, intermediate, and teeth-only exposure, plus a motion-to-display latency test. Our median TRE is 4.05, 6.10, and 7.10 mm respectively, and median latency is 0.805 s. These values support the feasibility of using AR to replace physical prints. The workflow removes mounted fiducials and manual landmark selection and provides a testable path toward transoral cutting guidance.

</details>


### [96] [SLED: Scalable Location Encoding via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06612)
*Kevin Lane,Zhongying Wang,Esther Rolf,Morteza Karimzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SLED，一种基于蒸馏的位置编码器框架，利用地理位置作为绑定模态，以极小批大小（128）高效预训练多模态地理空间表示模型，在19项人类中心基准任务上达到或超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的位置编码器依赖CLIP风格框架，需要16K-32K的大批大小，计算成本高昂，存在假负样本问题，且难以灵活扩展多模态数据。

Method: SLED采用蒸馏策略，将地理位置作为绑定模态，通过教师-学生蒸馏框架预训练位置编码器；支持任意模态的地理空间数据，无需时空共配准，模块化设计可灵活融合多模态信息。

Result: 在Sentinel-1、Sentinel-2和Landsat影像上预训练单模态和多模态SLED模型，在19项人类中心基准任务上与现有方法持平或超越，同时以128的批大小大幅降低运行时间和计算成本。

Conclusion: SLED证明了蒸馏式位置编码在效率和性能上的双重优势，消除大批次需求和样本共配准约束，为大规模地球观测数据的多模态表示学习提供了可扩展且经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The plethora of readily available geospatial data offers exciting opportunities to learn high quality representations of the planet, but the sheer size of the Earth Observations (EO), differing modalities, and different sensor types pose significant challenges in doing so. Location encoders have emerged as an efficient way of compressing EOs into location-specific embeddings. However, current state-of-the-art location encoders rely on computationally expensive CLIP-style frameworks that require large batch sizes in the 16K--32K range, suffer from false negative samples, and scale poorly with additional modalities. We introduce the Scalable Location Encoder via Distillation (SLED), a distillation-based location encoder that uses geospatial location as a binding modality to pretrain location encoders with any modality of geospatial data. The resulting location encoder framework is lightweight, modular, and can flexibly incorporate multiple modes, while eliminating the need for spatiotemporal coregistration of samples. SLED is performant with batch sizes as small as 128, enabling pretraining at a fraction of the runtime and compute costs of current state-of-the-art models. We demonstrate our approach by pretraining unimodal and multimodal SLED models on Sentinel-1, Sentinel-2, and Landsat imagery. We show that both unimodal and multimodal SLED models keep pace with or outperform existing approaches on a diverse set of 19 human-centric benchmark tasks and explore the benefits of using additional modes in pretraining.

</details>


### [97] [Do 3D Medical Foundation Models See Through MRI Artifacts? A Controlled Study of Representation Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06613)
*Julia Anna Mielcarz,Daniel Klaaby,Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种预训练3D医学影像基础模型对MRI伪影的鲁棒性，发现鲁棒性高度依赖模型和伪影类型：3DINO最稳定，BrainIAC最敏感，且大规模或领域特异性预训练并不能保证伪影不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督3D医学基础模型被广泛用作通用特征提取器，但其对MRI伪影的敏感性尚未被充分理解。在实际异构MRI环境中部署前，必须明确不同预训练策略是否具有伪影不变性。

Method: 使用BraTS-Africa数据集的四个MRI序列，对五个覆盖不同架构、目标函数、预训练领域和数据规模的3D编码器（3DINO、BrainIAC、NeuroVFM、BrainFM、Neuro-SimCLR）施加七种频率域和图像域伪影（每种五个强度级别），采用线性CKA、RankMe、UMAP评估表征鲁棒性，并结合独立的分割一致性分析进行交叉验证。

Result: 鲁棒性强烈依赖模型与伪影类型：3DINO表征最稳定，BrainIAC对多种伪影高度敏感，NeuroVFM、BrainFM和Neuro-SimCLR表现中等但各有独特的伪影敏感谱。多数条件下CKA大幅下降而RankMe相对稳定，说明伪影扭曲表征几何但未导致维度坍缩。分割一致性在伪影下退化（尤其重影和Rician噪声），但与表征级鲁棒性仅部分对齐。

Conclusion: 更大规模或领域特定的预训练并不能单独保证伪影不变性。在异构MRI环境中部署3D基础模型前，应进行显式的鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: Self-supervised 3D medical foundation models are increasingly used as general-purpose feature extractors, yet their sensitivity to MRI artifacts remains poorly understood. We present a controlled evaluation of representation robustness across five pretrained 3D encoders spanning different architectures, objectives, pretraining domains, and dataset scales. Using BraTS-Africa cases with four MRI sequences, we generate seven frequency- and image-domain artifacts at five predefined corruption settings. Robustness is assessed using linear centered kernel alignment (CKA), RankMe, and UMAP, complemented by an independent segmentation-consistency analysis. We find that robustness is strongly model- and artifact-dependent. 3DINO exhibits the most consistently stable representations, while BrainIAC is highly sensitive to several corruptions; NeuroVFM, BrainFM, and Neuro-SimCLR show intermediate but distinct artifact-specific profiles.
