Across many conditions, CKA decreases substantially while RankMe remains comparatively stable, indicating that artifacts often distort representation geometry without causing dimensional collapse. Segmentation consistency also degrades under corruption, particularly for ghosting and Rician noise, but aligns only partially with representation-level robustness. These findings show that larger-scale or domain-specific pretraining alone does not guarantee artifact invariance and motivate explicit robustness evaluation before deploying 3D foundation models in heterogeneous MRI settings.

</details>


### [98] [When Semantics Saturate or Emerge: Adaptation-Conditional Semantic Utility in Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06673)
*Wei Liu,Xing Deng,Haijian Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究在源域自由的跨域小样本学习（SF-CDFSL）中，基于冻结视觉-语言模型零样本准确率选择的语言描述，在目标域视觉适配（LoRA）后其排行是否仍然有效。通过严格配对实验发现两种模式：语义饱和（初始优势在大幅视觉适配后大幅收缩）和语义涌现（详细描述仅在视觉表示更新后才变得有用），证明零样本提示质量并非适配锚点质量的可靠代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SF-CDFSL方法通常依赖冻结VLM的零样本准确率来选择语言描述，但这一选择标准是否在目标域视觉适配后仍然成立尚未被系统验证。论文旨在揭示零样本提示质量与适配后语言有用性之间的可能脱节。

Method: 采用严格配对协议，在EuroSAT、CropDisease、ISIC和ChestX四个数据集上，分别使用通用类名模板和固定详细类描述作为语言输入，在视觉LoRA适配前后比较两者准确率差异（Δzero和Δlora）。通过训练轨迹、样本级分解、无不动点打乱语义对照、第二CLIP骨干和多种子实验进行验证。

Result: 发现两种稳定模式：语义饱和下（EuroSAT、CropDisease），初始8.13-21.54个百分点的优势在LoRA后收缩至0.69-2.96个百分点；语义涌现下（ISIC、ChestX），详细描述仅在适配后才显优势。饱和主要由Base-LoRA恢复详细语义已解决的错误驱动，涌现与预测翻转和新的Detailed-only正确决策相关。ChestX 1-shot为弱边界案例。

Conclusion: 零样本提示质量不能作为适配锚点质量的可靠代理，语言评估应在适配边界两侧（适配前后）同时进行，以更全面地衡量语言描述在SF-CDFSL中的实际价值。

Abstract: Language descriptions in source-free cross-domain few-shot learning (SF-CDFSL) are often selected according to zero-shot accuracy obtained with a frozen vision--language model. This paper asks whether that ranking remains valid after target-domain visual adaptation. Under a strictly paired protocol, we compare a generic class-name template with fixed detailed class descriptions before and after visual Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on EuroSAT, CropDisease, ISIC, and ChestX. Let $\deltazero$ and $\deltalora$ denote the Detailed-minus-Base accuracy before and after adaptation, respectively. Two recurring regimes emerge. In \emph{semantic saturation}, $\deltazero>0$ but $0<\deltalora\ll\deltazero$: on EuroSAT and CropDisease, initial gains of 8.13--21.54 percentage points contract to 0.69--2.96 points after LoRA. In \emph{semantic emergence}, $\deltazero\leq0$ but $\deltalora>0$: on ISIC and ChestX, detailed descriptions become more useful only after the visual representation is updated. Training trajectories and sample-level decomposition show that saturation is driven mainly by Base-LoRA recovering errors already solved by detailed semantics, whereas emergence is associated with prediction turnover and newly formed Detailed-only correct decisions. Fixed-point-free shuffled-semantic controls, a second CLIP backbone, and multiple random seeds support the broad pattern while identifying ChestX 1-shot as a weak boundary case. These findings establish that zero-shot prompt quality is an incomplete proxy for adaptation-anchor quality and motivate evaluating language on both sides of the adaptation boundary.

</details>


### [99] [Corrupting Attention: Evasion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Encoder Attention in Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06674)
*Ridma Jayasundara,Shaheer Mohamed,Tharindu Fernando,Harshala Gammulle,Basura Fernando,Sanka Rasnayake,A V Subramanyam,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个直接优化编码器注意力目标的对抗攻击方法，通过在不可感知的有界ℓ∞扰动下驱动模型自身注意力向损坏目标偏移，显著降低DETR系列目标检测器的性能（DETR-R50 mAP从42.1降至0.97，DINO-Swin-L从56.8降至1.44），建立密集与可变形注意力上的SOTA攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测中的Transformer（如DETR）已在安全关键系统中广泛部署，但其对抗鲁棒性研究不足。现有攻击大多针对检测输出而非注意力机制，而注意力机制恰恰是这类模型的核心特征。作者提出直接攻击编码器注意力，因为编码器注意力集中了模型的空间推理能力，破坏它将比破坏检测输出产生更严重的级联影响。

Method: 提出首个直接在不可感知的有界ℓ∞扰动下优化编码器注意力目标的对抗攻击。与以往通过可见patch引入攻击者自有sink token的方法不同，该方法驱动模型自身注意力向损坏目标偏移。论文验证了四种不同注意力损坏目标：分散（dispersion）、重排序（re-ranking）、置换（permutation）和峰值抑制（peak-suppression），以证明漏洞源于注意力结构本身的破坏而非特定目标。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，攻击将DETR-R50的mAP从42.1降至0.97，在相同扰动预算和迭代次数下比最强现有攻击产生的mAP降低约4倍。四种损坏目标下检测均降至3 mAP以下。攻击可迁移至不同注意力机制，将DINO-Swin-L从56.8降至1.44，而最强先前攻击仅降至7.3。

Conclusion: 编码器注意力是检测Transformer的一个系统性对抗弱点，破坏注意力结构本身（而非依赖特定损坏目标）即可有效地使检测性能崩溃。该漏洞在密集和可变形注意力机制上均广泛存在，凸显了注意力结构鲁棒性研究的重要性。

Abstract: Adversarial vulnerabilities remain a major concern for the safe deployment of neural networks, particularly in object detection, a core task embedded in many safety-critical systems. Detection transformers have emerged as leading object detectors, yet their adversarial robustness remains comparatively underexplored. Most existing attacks target the detection output rather than the attention mechanism that makes these models distinctive. In this paper, we introduce the first attack that directly optimizes an encoder-attention objective under an imperceptible, bounded $\ell_\infty$ perturbation. Rather than introducing an attacker-owned sink token through a visible patch, it drives the model's own attention toward a corrupted target. We argue that encoder attention concentrates the model's spatial reasoning, so corrupting it propagates through the detection pipeline more disruptively than perturbing the detection output alone. Our attack reduces DETR-R50 mAP on COCO from 42.1 to 0.97, a $\sim 4\times$ reduction in resulting mAP over the strongest existing attack under an identical perturbation budget and iteration count. We further show that this vulnerability is not specific to a particular corruption objective: across four qualitatively distinct targets, dispersion, re-ranking, permutation, and peak-suppression, detection consistently drops below 3 mAP, suggesting that the weakness arises from disrupting the attention structure itself rather than from any single target. Finally, we demonstrate that the attack generalizes across attention formulations, reducing DINO-Swin-L from 56.8 to 1.44 mAP against 7.3 for the strongest prior attack, establishing state-of-the-art on both dense and deformable attention.

</details>


### [100] [CoDAT: Collaborative Dual-Attention Transformer with Low-Cost Temporal Modeling for Efficient Edge Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06691)
*Novendra Setyawan,Chi-Chia Sun,Mao-Hsiu Hsu,Wen-Kai Kuo,Jing-Ming Guo,Jun-Wei Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDAT（协作双注意力Transformer），通过轻量级双分支模块（空间卷积注意力SCA + 步长单头注意力SSHA）替代传统多头注意力，并嵌入无参数TShift模块实现帧间时序通信，在边缘IoT设备上实现了人体动作识别的高效表现。在Jetson AGX Orin和Raspberry Pi 5上验证，CoDAT在ImageNet-1K、Kinetics-400、MA-52和UCF-101上均取得与SOTA方法相当或更优的精度与速度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D CNN、视频Transformer及Shift-based ViT虽然精度高，但计算开销过大，无法满足边缘IoT设备在延迟、内存和功耗方面的严苛限制。亟需一种既能捕获丰富的时空线索又能在严格资源约束下部署的实时人体动作识别模型。

Method: CoDAT的核心是双注意力架构：(1) 空间卷积注意力（SCA）负责局部特征聚合；(2) 步长单头注意力（SSHA）通过基于步长的稀疏投影同时压缩Q、K、V张量的空间分辨率和通道维度，以显著降低的计算成本捕获全局上下文；(3) 无参数的TShift模块嵌入每个block中，实现跨帧的时间通信。三者协作实现全局与局部特征的融合。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，CoDAT-M比EfficientViT384和FastViT-S12快2倍（精度相当）；CoDAT-L以3倍更少参数和2倍更高吞吐量匹配ViT-S。在Kinetics-400和MA-52上，CoDAT达到与SOTA基线竞争性的Top-1精度，速度比VSwin-T快2.9倍、比ViT-Temporal-Shift快2倍、比UniFormer-B快5倍。在UCF-101上，CoDAT-S384匹配TokShift和LAPS精度，但快6倍且FLOPs减少13倍。

Conclusion: CoDAT通过协作双注意力机制实现了边缘IoT实时动作识别中的效能平衡，验证了在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅降低计算成本的可能性，为边缘感知系统提供了一条实用的模型设计路径。

Abstract: Real-time human action recognition on Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge devices requires models that capture rich spatio-temporal cues within strict latency, memory, and power envelopes. Current 3D CNNs, video transformers, and shift-based ViT deliver high accuracy but come at computational costs that preclude edge IoT deployment. This paper proposes CoDAT, a Collaborative Dual-Attention Transformer that replaces conventional multi-head attention with a lightweight dual-branch module: Spatial Convolutional Attention (SCA) for local aggregation and Strided Single-Head Attention (SSHA) for global context. SSHA jointly compresses the spatial resolution and channel dimensions of the query, key, and value tensors via stride-based sparse projection, then fuses the resulting global and local features at a markedly reduced cost. To enable temporal communication across frames, a parameter-free TShift module is embedded in each block. Extensive experiments on Jetson AGX Orin and Raspberry Pi 5 demonstrate that CoDAT achieves an energy-accuracy balance in both image and action recognition. On ImageNet-1K, CoDAT-M runs 2x faster than EfficientViT384 and FastViT-S12 at comparable accuracy, and CoDAT-L matches ViT-S with 3x fewer parameters at 2x higher throughput. On Kinetics-400 and MA-52, CoDAT achieves competitive Top-1 accuracy against state-of-the-art CNN, transformer, and hybrid baselines while running up to 2.9x faster than VSwin-T, 2x faster than ViT-Temporal-Shift variants, and 5x faster than UniFormer-B. On UCF-101, CoDAT-S384 matches TokShift and LAPS while being 6x faster and requiring up to 13x fewer FLOPs, establishing an efficiency-accuracy balance for real-time action recognition in edge IoT perception systems. Code is available at https://github.com/novendrastywn/CoDAT .

</details>


### [101] [Suppress and Diversify: Refining Robust Pathways for Corruption Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06712)
*Jiangang Yang,Wenhui Shi,Xiaoran Xu,Wenyue Chong,Luqing Luo,Jing Xing,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统探索了内部鲁棒性的计算通路，发现鲁棒特征在网络层间逐渐衰减，并建立了特征丰度与模型性能间的函数依赖关系。据此提出Suppress & Diversify (S&D)方法，通过动态选择鲁棒通路并利用对称性保持变换进行多样化，实现架构无关、无参数、零测试开销的鲁棒性增强。在八个基准上的广泛评估验证了其跨任务、跨骨干网络和复杂真实场景的有效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要聚焦于隐式表示学习来提升模型对自然图像损坏的鲁棒性，缺少对内部鲁棒性计算通路的显式理解和系统探索，这对安全关键应用至关重要。

Method: 提出S&D (Suppress and Diversify)方法：动态选择鲁棒通路（suppress非鲁棒通路），并通过对称性保持变换（如对称变换）来多样化所选通路，增强特征的多样性。该方法是架构无关的（architecture-agnostic）、无参数的（parameter-free），且零测试开销。

Result: 在八个基准上进行了广泛评估，S&D在多种视觉任务、不同骨干网络以及复杂真实场景中均一致地提升了模型性能，验证了其广泛的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 内部鲁棒特征存在逐层衰减的规律且其丰度与性能存在函数依赖关系，S&D作为一种非侵入式的鲁棒性增强范式，通过显式调控鲁棒通路可以高效地提升模型鲁棒性，为鲁棒性研究提供了新的计算通路视角。

Abstract: Model robustness against natural image corruptions is essential for safety-critical applications. While existing methods primarily focus on implicit representation learning, we provide the first systematic exploration of computational pathways to explicitly characterize internal robustness. We identify a progressive decay of robust features across network layers and establish a functional dependency between the prevalence of these features and model performance. To exploit these insights, we propose Suppress and Diversify (S\&D), a non-intrusive refinement approach that enhances robustness by dynamically selecting robust pathways and diversifying them through symmetry-preserving transformations. S\&D is architecture-agnostic, parameter-free, and incurs zero test-time overhead. Extensive evaluations across eight benchmarks demonstrate that S\&D consistently improves performance across multiple vision tasks, diverse backbones, and complex real-world scenarios, highlighting its broad efficacy and scalability.

</details>


### [102] [WaveFreqAnchor: Wave-Structural Anchoring and Frequency Correction Diffusion for Training-Free Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06717)
*Zelin Du,Wenjie Li,Zhengxue Wang,Juncheng Li,Cailing Wang,Guangwei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaveFreqAnchor，一个无需训练的基于小波结构化锚定和频率校正扩散的人脸修复框架，通过ASWG约束面部结构、MWFI校正低频子带、SHE增强高频细节，在严重退化下保持身份一致性并恢复高质量面部细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的人脸修复方法在逆向扩散过程中约束不足，导致身份相关的结构漂移和严重退化下的保真度下降。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) Anchor-Space Wave-Structural Guidance (ASWG) 通过各向异性波响应一致性约束面部结构；(2) Multi-scale Wavelet-Fourier Injection (MWFI) 通过替换相位对齐预测低频子带与观测，校正逆向扩散中累积的不一致性；(3) Subband High-Frequency Enhancement (SHE) 对有界空间掩码的高频子带进行细化，恢复未知复合退化下的精细面部细节。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在保持面部身份的同时恢复清晰真实的面部细节，持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: WaveFreqAnchor通过小波域的结构锚定与频率校正双重机制，有效解决了扩散人脸修复中的身份漂移和细节保真问题，实现了无需训练的高质量高保真人脸修复。

Abstract: Diffusion-based face restoration that adjusts the sampling trajectory of pre-trained diffusion models has achieved remarkable progress. However, existing approaches provide insufficient constraints during reverse diffusion, causing identity-related structural drift and degraded fidelity under severe degradations. To address this, we propose WaveFreqAnchor, a training-free framework based on Wave-Structural Anchoring and Frequency Correction Diffusion. Specifically, Anchor-Space Wave-Structural Guidance (ASWG) constrains facial structures through anisotropic wave-response consistency, while Multi-scale Wavelet-Fourier Injection (MWFI) aligns the predicted low-frequency subband with the observation by replacing its phase, correcting inconsistencies accumulated during reverse diffusion. For real-world scenes, we further introduce Subband High-Frequency Enhancement (SHE), which performs bounded, spatially masked refinement on the predicted high-frequency subbands to recover fine facial details under unknown compound degradations. Together, these designs effectively preserve facial identity while restoring sharp and realistic facial details. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving high-quality and high-fidelity face restoration.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Starry Night: Shortcut-Aware Control-State Planning for Artist-Grounded Text to Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06751)
*Kuan Xing,Ye Wang,Changyi Gan,Yuheng Li,Thao Nguyen,Yi Chang,Yilin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Atelier，一个面向艺术家风格图像生成的"捷径感知控制状态规划"框架，将模糊的艺术意图转化为显式控制状态（包含场景锚点、保留/变换决策、风格时期假设、角色约束和捷径规避约束），并基于艺术家级知识与局部补丁参考进行生成，同时引入ArtIntentBench基准（涵盖梵高和齐白石）进行系统评估。结果表明相比基于提示工程、检索增强与通用智能体的基线，Atelier在风格保真度、结构保留和捷径替代抑制方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型对艺术家名字的响应往往依赖规范化的捷径（如反复出现的母题、通用调色板或过度代表性的时期特征），而非真正保留用户意图的场景。作者认为艺术家风格生成的关键瓶颈不仅在于图像合成本身，更在于上游对显式、有证据支撑的艺术控制的推断。

Method: Atelier框架包含四个核心环节：(1) 将未充分指定的艺术意图转化为显式控制状态，分离场景锚点、保留/变换决策、风格时期假设、角色绑定证据和捷径规避约束；(2) 利用艺术家级知识和局部图像补丁参考来佐证控制状态；(3) 编译后端感知的生成计划；(4) 通过全局与局部真实性反馈迭代优化候选生成结果。配套提出了ArtIntentBench基准，涵盖作品重渲染、时期/风格控制生成、历史未见主题、捷径审计和人类偏好评估等任务。

Result: 在开放权重和闭源生成器上，Atelier相比基于提示工程、检索增强和通用智能体的基线，在艺术家级风格保真度、源结构保留方面表现更优，并大幅减少捷径替代现象。

Conclusion: 研究表明艺术家风格生成的核心瓶颈不在图像合成技术本身，而在于上游层面对显式、证据驱动的艺术控制意图的推断能力，这为艺术风格生成领域指明了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Artist-grounded image generation requires more than appending an artist name to a prompt. Image models often respond to artist names through canonical shortcuts, such as recurring motifs, generic palettes, or overrepresented period signatures, rather than preserving the user's intended scene. We introduce Atelier, a shortcut-aware control-state planning framework for artist-grounded image generation. Atelier translates underspecified artistic intent into an explicit control state that separates scene anchors, preserve/transform decisions, style-regime hypotheses, role-bound artist evidence, and shortcut-avoidance constraints. It grounds this state using artist-level knowledge and local patch references, compiles backend-aware generation plans, and iteratively refines candidates through global and local authenticity feedback. We further introduce ArtIntentBench, a benchmark covering Van Gogh and Qi Baishi across artwork re-rendering, period/style-controlled generation, historically unseen subjects, shortcut auditing, and human preference evaluation. Across open-weight and closed-source generators, Atelier improves artist-level style fidelity, preserves source structure more faithfully, and substantially reduces shortcut substitution compared with prompt-engineered, retrieval-augmented, and general-purpose agent baselines. These results suggest that artist-grounded generation is bottlenecked not only by image synthesis, but by the upstream inference of explicit, evidence-grounded artistic controls.

</details>


### [104] [Explore or Converge? Stage-Guided Per-Step Optimization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06768)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGPO方法，通过信号-噪声比和语义变化识别扩散模型的生成阶段，并为不同阶段分配定制化优化目标，解决RL偏好对齐中的奖励稀疏与时间目标错配问题，实验显示26.7%生成质量提升和36.7%收敛速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的最大似然训练目标只关注重建数据分布，难以对齐特定偏好。虽然RL用于偏好对齐有前景，但存在奖励稀疏问题。现有方法将最终奖励反向传播至所有去噪步骤，但去噪是分阶段的，各阶段语义和可控性不同，导致时间目标错配、奖励捷径和奖励黑客问题，同时奖励回填使各时间步无法区分动作，削弱优化过程。

Method: 提出SGPO（Stage-Guided Per-Step Optimization）：联合利用信噪比（SNR）和语义变化来识别生成阶段，并自适应分配阶段特定目标。早期阶段注重退出混沌状态；中期阶段优化最终奖励同时探索多样性以避免过早收敛到单一模式；后期阶段注重稳定收敛以避免过拟合和质量退化。

Result: 在16组对比实验中，SGPO实现了26.7%的平均生成质量提升和36.7%的收敛速度提升。

Conclusion: 通过阶段感知的逐步骤优化策略，SGPO有效解决了奖励稀疏和奖励回填导致的优化弱化问题，显著提升扩散模型偏好对齐的生成质量与收敛效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models have strong generative capabilities. However, their maximum likelihood training objective only focuses on reconstructing the data distribution, making it difficult to align with specific preferences. Reinforcement learning (RL) for preference alignment in diffusion models is promising but limited by reward sparsity. Since a single reward cannot support optimization, existing RL methods usually backpropagate the final reward to all previous steps. However, denoising is stage-wise, with distinct semantics and controllability. Repeating the final reward across all steps creates a temporal objective mismatch, encouraging reward shortcuts that lead to reward hacking. At the same time, due to reward backfilling, each time step receives the same reward, making it impossible to distinguish between actions, thereby weakening the optimization process.
  To resolve this issue, we propose Stage-Guided Per-Step Optimization (SGPO) for diffusion models, which jointly leverages signal-to-noise ratio and semantic changes to identify generation stages and adaptively assign stage-specific objectives. Early denoising is chaotic and far from the final reward, resulting in weak reward-behavior correlation. This stage should prioritize exiting the chaotic state. In the mid stage, the latent transitions to a stable structure, where the final reward better corresponds to generative behavior. Therefore, this stage optimizes the final reward while exploring diversity to avoid early convergence to a single mode.
  In the late stage, the latent's core structure is largely fixed, and preference optimization mainly amplifies local details, risking overfitting. Therefore, stable convergence is preferred to avoid quality degradation. Results from 16 comparative experiments validate SGPO. Our method achieves 26.7% average gains in generative quality and 36.7% higher convergence speed.

</details>


### [105] [GraphVerse: A Comprehensive Visual Graph Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06769)
*Yuanfu Sun,Yuanhang Ren,Kang Li,Chuanhao Ji,Jiaxi Li,Jiajin Liu,Ninghao Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphVerse，一个统一的视觉图推理（VGR）基准，在单图和双图设置下联合评估MLLMs的感知、视觉推理和基于文本的图推理能力，并引入图中心图像编辑（GIE）策略和过程敏感的VGR-Score指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VGR基准存在多个局限：通常将任务简化为视觉感知后接文本推理、仅限单图设置、依赖仅答案正确性指标、且对真实图中心场景代表性不足。随着MLLMs在视觉-语言任务上的快速进步，迫切需要更具挑战性且能真正评估结构化视觉信息推理能力的基准。

Method: GraphVerse包含三部分：(1) 统一的评价框架，在单图和配对图像两种设置下联合评估感知、视觉推理和文本图推理；(2) 图中心图像编辑（GIE）策略，通过修改图图像同时保持语义不变，将其转化为积极的视觉推理测试；(3) VGR-Score，一种过程敏感的评估指标，衡量推理质量而不止于最终答案准确率。

Result: 大量实验揭示了当前MLLMs在VGR任务中的关键局限，同时验证了GIE策略的有效性以及GraphVerse向更广泛多模态推理能力迁移的可行性。

Conclusion: GraphVerse为MLLMs的视觉图推理能力提供了更全面和严格的评估基准，弥补了现有基准在设置、指标和场景代表性方面的不足，并有望推动多模态模型在结构化视觉推理上的进一步发展。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across diverse vision-language tasks, creating an urgent need for more challenging benchmarks. Yet existing evaluations still provide limited insight into whether these models can truly reason over structured visual information. Visual Graph Reasoning (VGR) offers a compelling testbed for this challenge, requiring models to integrate perception, structural understanding, and multi-step reasoning over graph-based visual inputs. However, prior VGR benchmarks often reduce the task to visual perception followed by text-based reasoning, restrict evaluation to single-image settings, rely on answer-only metrics, and underrepresent realistic graph-centric scenarios. To bridge the gap, we introduce GraphVerse, a unified benchmark that jointly evaluates perception, visual reasoning, and text-based graph reasoning in MLLMs under both single-image and paired-image settings. At its core is a suite of Graph-centric Image Editing (GIE) strategies that modify graph images while preserving their semantics, turning them into active tests of visual reasoning. We further propose VGR-Score, a process-sensitive metric that evaluates reasoning quality beyond final-answer accuracy. Extensive experiments reveal several key limitations of current MLLMs in VGR, while also validating the effectiveness of GIE strategies and the transferability of GraphVerse to broader multimodal reasoning capabilities. The code is available at https://github.com/sunyuanfu/GraphVerse.

</details>


### [106] [AnyTrack: Unifying Visual Object Tracking with Any Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06773)
*Hao Li,Yunzhi Zhuge,Wenning Hao,Pingping Zhang,Xiaoxiong Zhang,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnyTrack，一个统一的任意模态目标跟踪框架，通过Modality-aware Interaction Module（MIM）实现跨模态动态交互以维持时空一致性，并通过Context Understanding Module（CUM）利用全局-局部提示建立视觉特征与目标位置的空间对应关系。同时扩展了多模态基准以支持灰度图像、语言描述和音频等多样模态，在完整与缺失模态场景下均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态跟踪器通常针对固定的模态组合设计，需要为不同输入训练单独模型，导致对缺失或不完美模态的适应性差、泛化能力有限。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一个能处理任意模态组合的统一框架。

Method: 1) 提出Modality-aware Interaction Module（MIM），实现跨模态动态交互，弥合模态差异并聚合时序线索以维持时空一致性；2) 引入Context Understanding Module（CUM），通过全局-局部提示建立视觉特征与目标位置的空间对应关系，利用目标感知上下文建模增强前景-背景判别；3) 扩展现有基准，加入灰度图像、语言描述和音频剪辑以支持多模态训练与评估。

Result: 在完整与缺失模态设置下的大量实验表明，AnyTrack达到SOTA性能，验证了其有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: AnyTrack作为一个统一的多模态跟踪框架，能够有效适应不同模态组合及缺失模态场景，突破了传统固定模态组合方法的局限，为目标跟踪领域提供了更灵活和泛化能力更强的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual object tracking aims to continuously locate specific targets within sequential frames, evolving from single-modal methods to multi-modal ones. However, existing multi-modal trackers are typically designed for fixed modality combinations, requiring separate models for different inputs. This leads to a poor adaptability to missing or imperfect modalities, and limited generalization. To address these issues, we propose a novel unified framework called AnyTrack for object tracking with any modalities. Specifically, we design a Modality-aware Interaction Module (MIM) to facilitate dynamic interaction across diverse modalities. This module bridges modality discrepancies and aggregates temporal cues to maintain spatio-temporal consistency during cross-modal interaction. Furthermore, we introduce a Context Understanding Module (CUM) to establish spatial correspondence between visual features and target locations via global-local prompts. This module employs target-aware context modeling to enhance foreground-background discrimination for precise localization. Finally, to support the training and evaluation under diverse modalities, we extend existing multi-modal object tracking benchmarks by incorporating grayscale images, language descriptions, and audio clips. Extensive experiments with both complete and missing modality settings demonstrate that our AnyTrack achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its effectiveness and flexibility. The source code is available at https://github.com/IdolLab/AnyTrack.

</details>


### [107] [UniCycleFlow: Bidirectional Unpaired Image Translation with a Shared Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06784)
*Xianhao Zhou,Jianghao Wu,Shaoting Zhang,Guotai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCycleFlow提出了一种基于修正流（rectified-flow）的双向无配对图像翻译框架，将双向翻译建模为单一时间条件速度场的前向与反向积分，通过学习对抗匹配的确定性子源条件端点，结合stop-gradient自流匹配、离散循环闭合和路径速度正则化，在10个翻译任务中以单次Euler评估在7个任务上取得最低FID，平均FID达55.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双向无配对图像翻译方法通常使用两个方向特定的生成器或训练独立的单向模型，即使通过循环一致性连接，也只约束往返端点重建，不要求两个方向遵循共同的局部变换规则，缺乏将双向翻译统一在同一连续动力学框架中的机制。

Method: 将双向翻译表示为单一时间条件速度场的前向与反向积分，构建统一的连续动力学；针对无配对数据缺乏源-目标耦合的问题，学习确定性的源条件端点并通过对抗匹配使其边缘分布逼近目标域；采用stop-gradient自流匹配提供中间速度监督，离散循环闭合保证前向-反向一致性，表示路径速度正则化控制轨迹上的局部特征变化。

Result: 在十个翻译方向上，UniCycleFlow使用单次Euler评估在7/10任务上取得最低FID，并获得最佳平均FID 55.1。

Conclusion: 通过将双向翻译统一到单个速度场的连续动力学中，UniCycleFlow证明了一种更紧凑、更一致的框架能够在无配对双向翻译中取得优于分离双生成器方法的性能，表明共享动力学建模是提升双向翻译质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Bidirectional unpaired image translation must preserve source-specific structure while learning coherent transformations in both directions without paired supervision. Existing methods typically employ two direction-specific generators or train separate one-way models. Even when linked by cycle consistency, such models constrain only the round-trip endpoint reconstruction, without requiring the two directions to obey a common local transformation rule. We propose UniCycleFlow, a rectified-flow framework that represents bidirectional translation as forward and reverse integration of a single time-conditioned velocity field. This formulation organizes both directions within the same continuous dynamics, rather than coupling otherwise separate endpoint mappings. A key challenge is that unpaired data provide no meaningful source--target coupling from which rectified-flow trajectories can be constructed. UniCycleFlow addresses this challenge by learning deterministic source-conditioned endpoints whose marginal distributions are adversarially matched to the opposite domains. The resulting paths are regularized by stop-gradient self-flow matching for intermediate velocity supervision, discrete cycle closure for forward--reverse consistency, and representation path-velocity regularization for controlling localized feature changes along the trajectory. Across ten translation directions, UniCycleFlow achieves the lowest FID on 7 of 10 tasks using a single Euler evaluation and obtains the best average FID of 55.1.

</details>


### [108] [PAST: Prompt-Adaptive Sampling Termination for Efficient Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06794)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAST，一种通过内在奖励补偿稀疏外部奖励、动态感知去噪进度与提示难度来自适应调节训练回合长度的强化学习微调方法，将计算效率提升66.7%，偏好优化质量提升29.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在文生图任务中难以直接优化下游目标，现有RL方法存在微调效率低和奖励稀疏的问题。

Method: 1) 设计内在奖励范式补偿稀疏外部奖励并引导模型更高效地从噪声模式中探索偏离路径，并提供理论证明；2) 动态监测去噪完成度和图像结构与提示语义的对齐程度，满足条件时自适应终止训练，实现基于提示难度和生成过程的回合长度分配；3) 基于预测残差噪声水平建立双重自适应协调机制，同时平衡外部/内在奖励以及探索/收敛。

Result: PAST将现有RL微调方法的计算效率提升高达66.7%，并通过双重自适应调节机制将偏好优化质量提升高达29.5%。

Conclusion: PAST通过内在奖励补偿和自适应回合长度调控，有效解决了扩散模型RL微调中效率低和奖励稀疏的问题，显著提升了计算效率和优化质量。

Abstract: While diffusion models have made significant progress in text-to-image tasks, they still exhibit limitations when directly optimizing downstream objectives. Although Reinforcement Learning (RL) enables targeted optimization, existing methods are generally constrained by low-efficiency fine-tuning and sparse rewards. To address these challenges, we propose PAST, which provides differentiated rewards while adaptively regulating training episode length by jointly perceiving denoising progress and prompt difficulty. Specifically, we design an intrinsic reward paradigm to compensate for sparse extrinsic rewards and guide the model to explore paths that diverge more efficiently from noise patterns. We further provide theoretical justification for intrinsic rewards. Then, PAST dynamically monitors denoising completion and semantic alignment between image structures and prompt semantics. When both metrics satisfy generation requirements, the system adaptively terminates training. This enables appropriate allocation of episode lengths based on prompt difficulty and the current generation process. Finally, based on the predicted residual noise level, we establish a dual adaptive coordination mechanism. Specifically, it not only balances the extrinsic and intrinsic rewards but also balances the exploration and convergence. Experimental results demonstrate that PAST enhances computational efficiency of existing RL fine-tuning methods by up to 66.7%, while improving preference optimization quality by up to 29.5% through its dual adaptive regulation mechanism.

</details>


### [109] [AdvTiles: Physical Adversarial Camouflage Clothing against Person Detectors via Learnable Tiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06801)
*Jinlei Wang,Jiahuan Long,Mingkai Sun,Yafei Guo,Yuanhao Huang,Ming Wang,Junqi Wu,Jiacheng Hou,Hongbo Chen,Xingxing Wei,Tingsong Jiang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdvTiles，一种基于可学习瓦片的物理对抗伪装框架，通过ST Gumbel-Softmax进行可微分的瓦片选择和布局优化，配合3D高斯溅射渲染增强物理鲁棒性，在多个检测器上达到86.2%的平均攻击成功率（ASR），并成功制作可穿戴衣物在真实场景中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理对抗攻击方法从局部补丁发展到全身纹理，但难以同时实现视觉自然性和强攻击效果。现有自然外观方法通常整体优化伪装纹理，缺乏对局部对抗模式及其空间排列的精细控制能力。

Method: 1) 提出AdvTiles框架，由可学习瓦片构成对抗伪装；2) 使用Straight-through (ST) Gumbel-Softmax估计器实现可微分的瓦片选择，联合优化瓦片图案与空间布局；3) 通过可微分的3D高斯溅射（3D Gaussian Splatting）渲染，在不同视角、尺度、光照和背景下优化伪装，提升物理条件的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个检测器上的大量实验表明，AdvTiles达到86.2%的平均攻击成功率（ASR），优于现有最先进攻击方法。此外，将优化后的伪装制作成可穿戴对抗衣物，在真实场景中跨越不同距离、角度和背景验证了有效性。

Conclusion: AdvTiles通过可学习瓦片的细粒度优化，成功在保持自然伪装外观的同时实现强攻击性能，并通过3D渲染增强物理鲁棒性，在虚拟和真实场景中均验证了方法的优越性。

Abstract: Physical adversarial attacks against person detectors have evolved from localized patches to full-body textures. However, achieving both visual naturalness and strong attack effectiveness remains challenging. Existing natural-looking methods typically optimize camouflage textures as a whole, limiting the flexibility to refine local adversarial patterns and their spatial arrangement. To address this issue, we propose AdvTiles, a physical adversarial camouflage framework built from learnable tiles, enabling strong attack performance while preserving a natural camouflage appearance. Specifically, we use a Straight-through (ST) Gumbel-Softmax estimator for differentiable tile selection, enabling joint optimization of tile patterns and spatial layouts. This design provides fine-grained control over adversarial texture generation. To improve robustness in diverse physical conditions, we further optimize the camouflage through differentiable 3D Gaussian Splatting rendering with variations in viewpoints, scales, illuminations and backgrounds. Extensive experiments across multiple detectors demonstrate that AdvTiles achieves an average ASR of 86.2%, outperforming existing state-of-the-art attack methods. We further fabricate the optimized camouflage into wearable adversarial clothing, validating its effectiveness in real-world scenarios across diverse distances, angles and backgrounds.

</details>


### [110] [Bend the Basics: Degradation-Aware Deformable Tokenization for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06832)
*Zihao He,Yunfeng Wu,Xinchao Wang,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Flexible Image Transformer (FIT)，一种全流程显式建模退化感知的图像修复统一框架，通过轻量退化编码器预测全局退化向量和空间退化图，自适应地调节patch嵌入和重建（变形），并引入task-token dropout增强跨退化类型鲁棒性。在5个标准基准上达到SOTA，五退化设置平均PSNR 30.72 dB，三退化设置32.83 dB，比现有统一修复方法高+0.5~1.1 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一图像修复Transformer大多依赖固定的patch划分，退化/任务条件仅在token化后的backbone块中注入，导致嵌入和重建阶段对局部退化变化不敏感，无法有效应对空间非均匀的多种退化。作者希望在整个pipeline（从patch采样到像素重建）中显式建模退化感知，以提升统一修复的性能和适应性。

Method: FIT包含三个关键设计：(1) 轻量退化编码器(Degradation Encoder)，从局部退化严重性预测全局退化向量g和空间退化图M；(2) 利用g和M联合条件化patch嵌入和解嵌入（unembedding），通过自适应变形(adaptive deformation)实现退化感知的token化与重建；(3) 引入task-token dropout策略，在训练期间对任务条件进行正则化，提升模型跨退化类型的鲁棒性。

Result: 在BSD68、Rain100L、SOTS、GoPro和LOLv1五个标准基准上，五退化设置下平均PSNR达30.72 dB，三退化设置下达32.83 dB，超过近期统一修复方法+0.5~1.1 dB。此外，学习到的偏移量为退化感知的空间自适应提供了直接的可视化抓手。

Conclusion: FIT通过将退化感知从backbone扩展到嵌入和重建阶段，打破了传统统一Transformer仅依赖固定patch划分的限制，在多种退化类型和空间非均匀退化场景下均取得优异性能，并提供了可解释的退化自适应可视化分析。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration seeks a single model that can recover images degraded by diverse and spatially non-uniform corruptions. However, many unified Transformers rely on fixed patch partitioning: task/degradation condition is injected only into the backbone blocks after tokenization, leaving the embedding and reconstruction stages insensitive to local degradation variations. In contrast to previous approaches, we present Flexible Image Transformer (FIT) that explicitly models degradation awareness across the entire pipeline, from patch sampling to pixel reconstruction. Specifically, FIT employs a lightweight Degradation Encoder to predict a global degradation vector $\mathbf{g}$ and a spatial degradation map $\mathbf{M}$ from local degradation severity, which jointly condition the patch embedding and unembedding through adaptive deformation. Moreover, to improve robustness across degradation types, we introduce a task-token dropout strategy that regularizes task conditioning during training. On five standard benchmarks (BSD68, Rain100L, SOTS, GoPro, and LOLv1), FIT achieves state-of-the-art performance with 30.72 dB average PSNR on the five-degradation setting and 32.83 dB on the three-degradation setting, outperforming recent unified restoration methods by +0.5$\sim$1.1 dB. Moreover, the learned offsets provide a direct handle for visualizing degradation-aware spatial adaptation.

</details>


### [111] [GOPI: Generation-Oriented 3D Pose Inference for Furniture Insertion from Single-View RGB-D Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06836)
*Ruifeng Zhai,Renjie Liu,Guangrun Wang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架解决单视图室内场景中的家具插入问题：首先通过GOPI进行数据驱动的3D位姿推断以解决单视图条件下物理尺度不可确定的问题，然后通过几何引导的条件生成策略将推断的3D位姿投影到图像平面作为像素级约束，保证生成图像与3D几何的一致性。实验验证了该方法在3D位姿估计和图像合成两方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单视图2D图像平面条件下插入新家具时，家具相对于场景的物理尺度无法被唯一确定，使得基于图像证据的物理合理家具放置本质上是不适定问题。因此需要将任务重新表述为3D位姿推断与几何引导图像生成的组合，其中估计几何上合理的3D放置对可靠的图像合成至关重要。

Method: 本文提出两阶段框架：(1) GOPI——一种面向生成的3D位姿推断框架，通过数据驱动的迭代推断解决单视图家具插入中固有的不适定问题，生成几何上合理的物体放置；(2) 几何引导的条件生成策略——将推断的3D位姿投影到图像平面作为像素对齐约束，确保合成图像与底层3D几何之间的一致性。

Result: 在3D位姿估计方面，GOPI相比直接回归和vanilla基线产生更强几何可行性和与参考布局更好一致性的位姿；在图像合成方面，该方法在不同家具尺度下均保持与投影3D几何的对齐，展现出跨尺度的稳定投影-生成对齐性能。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段框架有效解决了单视图家具插入中的尺度不确定性问题，通过将3D位姿推断与几何引导的图像生成相结合，实现了既符合物理几何约束又保持图像视觉一致的家具插入效果。

Abstract: We study the problem of inserting new furniture into indoor scene images. Under masked single-view 2D image-plane conditioning, however, the physical scale of the inserted furniture relative to the scene cannot be uniquely determined, making physically grounded furniture placement underdetermined from image evidence alone. We therefore reformulate the task as a combination of 3D pose inference and geometry-guided image generation, where estimating a geometrically plausible 3D placement is essential for reliable synthesis.
  To this end, we propose a two-stage framework. For 3D placement, we introduce GOPI, a generation-oriented 3D pose inference framework that addresses the underdetermined nature of single-view furniture insertion through data-driven iterative inference, producing geometrically plausible object placements. For image generation, we develop a geometry-guided conditioning strategy that projects the inferred 3D pose into the image plane as a pixel-aligned constraint, enforcing consistency between the synthesized image and the underlying 3D geometry.
  Experimental results validate the proposed framework from both 3D pose estimation and image synthesis perspectives. For 3D placement, GOPI produces poses with stronger geometric feasibility and better consistency with reference layouts than direct regression and vanilla baselines. For image synthesis, our method preserves alignment with the projected 3D geometry across different furniture scales, showing stable projection-generation alignment across the tested furniture scales.

</details>


### [112] [ECAD: Expanding Class-Agnostic Detection Beyond Thing-Centric Objectness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06841)
*Liang Wan,Zixin Ren,Yupeng Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Fangzhuo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展类无关检测（ECAD）新任务，旨在超越传统"物-中心"检测范式，发现天空、道路、草地等非可数视觉元素的候选区域。作者构建了BTCO-Bench基准，并提出基于冻结DINOv3编码器的轻量DETR检测器ECADet，通过几何感知专家回归（GAER）和原型引导查询调制（PGQM）提升定位与对象性估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测范式大多继承了以"物"为中心的对象性概念，仅定位离散可数的物体实例，导致天空、道路、草地等语义重要的视觉元素被吸收到背景中，影响了场景理解与空间推理能力。因此需要扩展检测范式以覆盖超越传统物体概念的视觉候选。

Method: 提出ECAD新任务与BTCO-Bench基准（含真实与跨域场景的类别无关框标注）；提出ECADet，基于冻结的DINOv3编码器的轻量DETR架构，并引入两个关键模块：Geometry-Aware Expert Regression (GAER) 改善多样化视觉元素的定位，Prototype-Guided Query Modulation (PGQM) 改善对象性估计。

Result: 在BTCO-Bench上，ECADet在多项指标上一致优于代表性类别无关检测器和基于提议的检测器，验证了扩展对象性发现的有效性。代码与基准将发布。

Conclusion: 通过将检测目标从"物-中心"扩展到更广泛的类别无关视觉元素，可以显著提升对场景中非可数但语义重要的区域的感知能力，为场景理解与空间推理提供更完整的视觉表征支持。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental task in visual perception, providing structured region representations for recognition, grounding, reasoning, and interaction. However, existing detection paradigms largely inherit a thing-centric notion of objectness, where detectors are mainly trained to localize discrete and countable object instances. Consequently, many semantically meaningful visual elements, such as sky, road, grassland, water, and sports courts, are often absorbed into the background despite their importance for scene understanding and spatial reasoning. In this paper, we formulate Expanded Class-Agnostic Detection (ECAD), a new setting that aims to discover category-agnostic visual candidates beyond conventional thing-centric objects. To support this setting, we construct BTCO-Bench, a Beyond Thing-Centric Objectness benchmark with category-agnostic box annotations covering both real-world and cross-domain scenarios. We further propose ECADet, a lightweight DETR-based detector built upon a frozen DINOv3 encoder, and introduce Geometry-Aware Expert Regression (GAER) and Prototype-Guided Query Modulation (PGQM) to improve localization and objectness estimation for diverse visual elements, respectively. Extensive experiments show that ECADet consistently outperforms representative class-agnostic and proposal-based detectors on BTCO-Bench, demonstrating the effectiveness of expanded objectness discovery. Code and benchmark will be released.

</details>


### [113] [RegionDet: A Benchmark for Region Detection Beyond Object Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06850)
*Liang Wan,Yuhan Wang,Yupeng Zhang,Zhen Xu,Han Wang,Fangjie Fu,Sirui Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Region Detection任务，将目标检测从离散目标实例扩展到区域目标（如施工区、排队、交谈等），并构建包含8个类别的RegionDet基准，系统性评估了闭集和开放词汇检测器，发现现有检测器尤其是视觉-语言检测器存在严重的物体中心偏差，难以处理弱边界、强上下文依赖的区域目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中许多视觉目标并非独立物体实例，而是由视觉状态、场景上下文、物体关系和人类活动定义的区域（如施工区、受损道路、排队、群聊等）。现有检测基准主要围绕物体实例构建，缺乏对此类区域目标的系统评估支持，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了Region Detection任务定义，将传统物体检测扩展至区域目标；构建了RegionDet基准，包含Construction、Crossing、Damage、Queuing、Talking、Vendor、Waiting、Walking八个区域类别，采用COCO风格边界框标注和评估协议；系统评估了代表性闭集检测器和零样本/开放词汇检测器。

Result: 闭集检测器在监督下能部分学习区域级模式，而零样本/开放词汇检测器表现严重不佳，揭示了当前视觉-语言检测器强烈的物体中心偏差。进一步分析指出了Region Detection的关键挑战：弱边界线索、强上下文依赖、以及关系级区域理解不足。

Conclusion: RegionDet基准将公开发布，为区域目标定位研究提供系统评估平台，同时也暴露出现有检测范式在区域级推理上的局限性，呼吁社区关注并解决这一重要但被忽视的检测任务。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental task in computer vision and has achieved remarkable progress on standard benchmarks by localizing discrete and well-bounded object instances. However, many visual targets in real-world scenarios are not individual objects, but regions defined by visual states, scene context, object relations, and human activities, such as construction areas, damaged road regions, queues, group conversations, and vendor regions. Existing detection benchmarks are mainly built around object instances, providing limited support for systematically evaluating such region targets. To address this gap, we introduce Region Detection, a task that extends conventional object detection beyond object instances, and construct RegionDet, a benchmark for region target localization. RegionDet contains eight region categories, including Construction, Crossing, Damage, Queuing, Talking, Vendor, Waiting, and Walking, with COCO-style bounding-box annotations and evaluation protocols. We systematically evaluate representative closed-set and zero-shot/open-vocabulary detectors on RegionDet. Results show that closed-set detectors can partially learn region-level patterns under supervision, while zero-shot/open-vocabulary detectors struggle severely, revealing the strong object-centric bias of current vision-language detectors. Further analyses highlight key challenges in Region Detection, including weak boundary cues, strong context dependency, and insufficient relation-level region understanding. The RegionDet will be released.

</details>


### [114] [Multi-Agent Forensic Reasoning for Generalizable Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06865)
*Xuechao Zou,Shun Zhang,Kai Li,Yi Zhou,Xinyu Sun,Yuhui Chen,Zhe Wu,Congyan Lang,Junliang Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文引入FaceVid-Forensics-100K,一个包含10万视频、覆盖33种合成方法的大型深度伪造视频基准数据集,并提出一种基于多智能体的取证推理框架(ARGUS),通过四个领域专家智能体从纹理、光照、运动和物理四个角度独立分析伪造痕迹,再由法官智能体综合判断。该框架仅使用小型开源MLLM即可在所有指标上超越包括闭源GPT和Gemini在内的所有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造视频基准对最新合成方法覆盖有限且缺乏细粒度的文本标注;传统检测器与多模态大语言模型(无论是单模型还是单一分析视角)难以捕捉细微伪造痕迹,泛化能力受限,亟需更全面的基准和更鲁棒的检测框架。

Method: (1)构建FaceVid-Forensics-100K数据集,包含10万视频、33种合成方法(涵盖换脸、面部重演和全脸合成,含Seedance 2.0等最新生成器),通过先进MLLM驱动的多模型聚合与冲突消解流水线自动生成细粒度文本标注(视觉观察和与判决一致的取证解释)。(2)提出多智能体取证推理框架(ARGUS),由四个专业领域专家智能体(纹理、光照、运动、物理)独立分析伪造线索,再由一个法官智能体整合各报告给出最终预测及解释。

Result: 在域外测试集上的广泛评估表明,尽管仅由小型开源MLLM组成,ARGUS框架在所有报告指标上均优于包括闭源GPT和Gemini模型在内的所有方法,排名第一。

Conclusion: 多智能体分工协作的取证推理范式能有效提升深度伪造视频检测的泛化能力,即使在资源受限的小模型条件下也能超越大规模闭源模型;同时完善的基准数据集对于推动该领域研究至关重要。

Abstract: The malicious use of generative artificial intelligence to create highly realistic deepfake videos raises serious ethical concerns and poses substantial challenges to AI safety. However, existing deepfake video benchmarks provide limited coverage of recent synthesis methods and generally lack reliable fine-grained textual annotations. Meanwhile, conventional detectors and multimodal large language models (MLLMs), whether operating as a single model or relying on a single analytical perspective, often fail to capture subtle forgery artifacts, limiting their generalization to emerging AI-generated methods. To address these limitations, we introduce FaceVid-Forensics-100K, a large-scale deepfake video dataset comprising 100,000 videos and spanning 33 synthesis methods across face swapping, face reenactment, and entire-face synthesis, including recent generators such as Seedance 2.0. The dataset provides fine-grained textual annotations of visual observations and verdict-consistent forensic explanations, automatically synthesized through a multi-model aggregation and conflict-resolution pipeline powered by advanced MLLMs. Building on this benchmark, we propose a multi-agent forensic reasoning framework that employs four specialized domain-expert agents to independently analyze forgery cues from four perspectives: texture, lighting, motion, and physics. A judge agent then reconciles their reports to produce a final prediction together with an explanation. Extensive evaluations on out-of-domain test sets show that, despite being composed entirely of small open-source MLLMs, our framework outperforms all methods including closed-source GPT and Gemini models and ranks first across all reported metrics on this benchmark. The project page is available at https://xavierjiezou.github.io/ARGUS/.

</details>


### [115] [DAEP: Difficulty-Aware Evidence Planning for Medical Video Corpus Temporal Answer Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06869)
*Tianjian He,Yujie Liu,Zhiping Huang,Changbo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAEP是BIGC团队提交给NLPCC 2026共享任务（DA-TAGVC）的系统，通过将难度标签转化为推理时证据规划（控制模态权重、Top-K聚合、边界阈值等），在50个候选视频中检索目标视频并定位答案支撑区间，最终以0.2728的Average score在十个系统中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DA-TAGVC任务要求从50个候选视频中检索目标视频并定位答案支撑片段，具有难度差异（简单/复杂问题标签）。作者旨在利用任务提供的难度标签，通过难度感知的推理规划来提升视频问答中跨模态证据融合与时间定位的性能。

Method: DAEP采用两阶段流水线：(1) 视频排名阶段，综合字幕、视觉和过程上下文证据对50个候选视频进行排序；(2) 时间跨度生成与重排序阶段，从高置信度的锚点扩展为时间跨度，并进行重排序输出。核心创新是将简单/复杂输入标签转化为推理时的证据规划，动态调节模态权重、Top-K聚合数量、边界阈值、扩展长度和重排序强度。

Result: 在官方评测中，BIGC在十个参赛系统中排名第一，Average score为0.2728。消融实验验证了视觉证据、过程上下文和难度感知规划均能提升排序质量，其中对复杂问题的增益最大。

Conclusion: DAEP验证了难度感知的推理时规划策略在多模态视频时序问答中的有效性，特别是对复杂问题的显著提升，表明将任务元信息（难度标签）动态融入模型推理过程是一种有效的设计方向。

Abstract: We describe DAEP, team BIGC's submission to NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 1 Track 3: Difficulty-Aware Temporal Answer Grounding in Video Corpus (DA-TAGVC). The task requires retrieving the target video from 50 candidates and localizing the answer-supporting span. DAEP ranks videos with subtitle, visual, and procedural-context evidence, expands high-scoring anchors into temporal spans, and reranks spans for final output. Its main design is to convert the task-provided simple/complex input label into an inference-time evidence plan controlling modality weights, Top-K aggregation, boundary threshold, expansion length, and reranking strength. In the official evaluation, BIGC ranks first among ten systems with an Average score of 0.2728. Validation ablations show that visual evidence, procedural context, and difficulty-aware planning improve ranking quality, with the largest gain on complex questions.

</details>


### [116] [FedVAR: Prototype-Aligned Federated Framework for Video Anomaly Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06876)
*Ghani Haider,Majid Kundroo,Boyun Eom,Dong Hwan Park,Chen Chen,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedVAR，一个基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）原型对齐机制的弱监督联邦学习框架，专门针对视频异常识别（VAR）中的语义错位问题，通过在分布式客户端间建立共享语义锚点来对齐视觉和文本特征空间，实现细粒度异常识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在IIoT和CPS时代，联邦学习为视频异常识别提供了去中心化智能方案，但分布式客户端间的数据异构性导致"语义错位"问题——各客户端对"正常"和"异常"事件学习到不同的特征表示。现有联邦方法主要关注二元异常检测，无法解决这一错位问题，阻碍了有效的细粒度识别。

Method: FedVAR利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的丰富表示，采用基于原型的对齐机制为所有客户端创建共享语义锚点，用于重新居中并对齐各客户端的视觉和文本特征空间。该过程强制在网络中形成一致的"正常性"表示，直接缓解语义错位，并以极低的通信开销实现异常方向向量的鲁棒提示学习。

Result: 在多种非IID数据划分方案、未见域和新型异常类别下进行了大量基准实验，结果表明FedVAR始终优于最先进的联邦基线方法。

Conclusion: FedVAR为基于视频的CPS中的分布式智能建立了稳健框架，通过原型对齐有效解决了语义错位问题，实现了跨客户端一致的细粒度视频异常识别。

Abstract: In the era of Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) and Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS), Federated Learning (FL) offers a promising decentralized intelligence paradigm for Video Anomaly Recognition (VAR). This task is vital for maintaining high-fidelity Digital Twins and ensuring safety in mission-critical environments. However, the inherent data heterogeneity across distributed edge clients leads to a fundamental challenge known as semantic misalignment, where clients learn divergent feature representations of "normal" and "abnormal" events. The problem becomes particularly pronounced in VAR, where the presence of diverse and fine-grained anomaly categories leads each client to develop distinct semantic interpretations of abnormality. Existing federated methods primarily focus on binary anomaly detection and fail to address this misalignment, preventing effective fine-grained recognition. In this paper, we introduce FedVAR, a weakly-supervised FL framework explicitly designed for VAR. Leveraging the rich representations of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), FedVAR employs a prototype-based alignment mechanism that creates a shared semantic anchor for all clients to re-center and align their visual and textual feature spaces. This process enforces a consistent representation of "normality" across the decentralized network, directly mitigating semantic misalignment and enabling robust prompt-learning of anomaly direction vectors with minimal communication overhead. We conduct extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks under various non-IID data partitioning schemes, unseen domains, and novel anomaly classes. The results demonstrate that FedVAR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art federated baselines, establishing a robust framework for distributed intelligence in video-based CPS.

</details>


### [117] [ControlRef: Efficient Layout-Guided Multi-Instance Generation via Anchored 4D-RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06878)
*Yunkai Yang,Yudong Zhang,Xinying Chen,Haoyuan Liang,Yizhuo Niu,Jinshuai Cheng,Kunquan Zhang,Liziyue Fang,Weitao Wan,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ControlRef 是一个面向 MM-DiT 的高效多实例图像生成框架，通过 UILC 注意力掩码和 Anchored 4D-RoPE 位置编码，解决了现有方法在计算开销和空间-频率折衷上的问题，在保持 SOTA 视觉质量的同时将推理延迟降低 80% 以上、内存开销降低 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统一的多模态扩散 Transformer（MM-DiTs）架构中实现可控的多实例图像生成仍具挑战。现有方法依赖全分辨率画布填充和 Shifted-RoPE 来管理多参考图像，这不仅在稀疏布局下大幅增加计算开销，还会破坏 RoPE 中的低频特征，导致绝对空间对应关系模糊，产生严重的空间-频率折衷问题。

Method: ControlRef 提出两个核心组件：(1) Unified Instance-Layout Control (UILC) 注意力掩码，用于严格解耦实例间的语义交互并强制精确的区域绑定；(2) Anchored 4D-RoPE，一种新型位置编码机制，将 token 直接锚定到其绝对几何中心。该方法通过将参考图像预对齐到对应边界框分辨率、将布局和参考 token 物理锚定到绝对几何中心、并沿 z 轴堆叠参考图像，从而保留空间先验，避免了有损移位带来的空间-频率妥协。

Result: 大量实验表明，ControlRef 在视觉保真度和定位精度上达到 SOTA，同时在稀疏布局下将推理延迟降低超过 80%，在密集场景中将内存开销减少 50%。

Conclusion: ControlRef 通过创新的掩码机制和锚定式位置编码，成功在统一架构中实现了高效且精确的多实例可控生成，有效解决了空间-频率折衷问题，为该方向提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Layout-guided multi-instance generation is essential for controllable image synthesis in Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs). However, integrating this capability into unified architectures remains challenging. Prior frameworks rely on redundant full-resolution canvas padding and Shifted-RoPE to manage multiple reference images. This mechanism drastically inflates computational overhead for sparse layouts and disrupts critical low-frequency RoPE features, creating a severe spatial-frequency compromise that blurs absolute spatial correspondence. To overcome these limitations, we propose ControlRef, a highly efficient and precise multi-instance synthesis framework. ControlRef utilizes a Unified Instance-Layout Control (UILC) attention mask to strictly decouple inter-instance semantic interactions and enforce precise regional binding. To further promote region-level spatial alignment, we introduce Anchored 4D-RoPE, a novel positional encoding mechanism that directly anchors tokens to their absolute geometric centers. By pre-aligning reference images to their corresponding bounding box resolutions, physically anchoring both layout and reference tokens to their absolute geometric centers, and stacking the references along the z-axis, Anchored 4D-RoPE natively preserves spatial priors and mitigates the spatial-frequency compromise without lossy shifting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ControlRef achieves state-of-the-art visual fidelity and localization accuracy, while concurrently slashing inference latency by over 80% in sparse layouts and reducing memory overhead by 50% in dense scenarios.

</details>


### [118] [HazeSpikeMamba: Coupling Spiking-Inspired and State-Space Features for Self-Supervised Real-World Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06886)
*Haoran Liu,Huibin Li,Mingzhe Liu,Peng Li,Guibin Zan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HazeSpikeMamba，一个紧凑的去雾框架，结合尖峰启发的局部路径（基于PCNN邻域耦合的TPCNNSpike方案）和注意力状态空间全局路径（基于MambaIRv2的Attentive State-Space Module），采用多尺度U-Net结构。针对真实照片去雾性能下降问题，该方法利用冻结的退化网络从去雾预测中重新合成雾霾，仅更新最终恢复层实现无标签的转导式目标域自适应。模型仅含2.02M活跃参数，在RTTS、URHI和HSTS数据集上持续改善BRISQUE和NIMA指标（RTTS上BRISQUE从30.13降至27.72，NIMA从4.13升至4.87），在转导协议下达到最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去雾网络通常在合成雾霾-清晰对上训练，但由于大气散射模型无法充分捕捉真实雾霾的变异性，且真实配对数据稀缺，模型在真实照片上性能往往下降。需要一种能够有效进行目标域自适应且不依赖配对真实数据的去雾方法。

Method: HazeSpikeMamba采用多尺度U-Net架构，包含两条路径：(1) 局部路径使用TPCNNSpike，一种受PCNN邻域耦合启发的尖峰发射方案，通过高斯加权邻域的前一发射状态并行更新所有神经元，替代分组定向扫描；(2) 全局路径采用MambaIRv2的Attentive State-Space Module（保留语义提示和序列重排，移除窗口自注意力分支），以序列长度线性的复杂度建模长程依赖。自适应阶段使用冻结在NH-HAZE上预训练的退化网络从去雾预测中重新合成雾霾，通过重构误差仅更新最终恢复层，无需无雾标签。采用数据集级别的转导式评估协议。

Result: 模型包含2.02M活跃参数，在256x256输入下需13.27G名义MACs。自适应一致改善了RTTS、URHI和HSTS上的BRISQUE和NIMA指标。RTTS上BRISQUE从30.13降至27.72，NIMA从4.13升至4.87。在该转导协议下，自适应后的模型在URHI和HSTS上均取得最优BRISQUE和NIMA。

Conclusion: HazeSpikeMamba证明了一种紧凑的混合架构（尖峰局部建模+状态空间全局建模）配合基于退化网络重合成的转导式自适应策略，可以在无需配对真实数据的情况下有效提升真实场景去雾质量，且评估指标（BRISQUE、NIMA）显著优于对比方法。

Abstract: Dehazing networks are commonly trained on synthetic hazy-clear pairs, but their performance often drops on real photographs. Synthetic haze generated using the atmospheric scattering model does not fully capture the variability of real haze, and paired real hazy-clear images are scarce. In this work, we propose HazeSpikeMamba, a compact dehazing framework that combines a spiking-inspired local path and an attentive state-space global path in a multi-scale U-Net. The local path uses TPCNNSpike, a new spike-emission scheme inspired by the neighborhood coupling of Pulse-Coupled Neural Network (PCNN). Unlike grouped directional scanning, TPCNNSpike updates all neurons in parallel using the previous firing states of their Gaussian-weighted neighborhoods. The global path adapts the Attentive State-Space Module of MambaIRv2, retaining semantic prompting and sequence reordering while removing the window self-attention branch. Its state-space processing models long-range dependencies with complexity linear in sequence length. For target-domain adaptation, a frozen degradation network, pretrained on paired NH-HAZE data, re-synthesizes haze from the dehazed prediction. The reconstruction error updates only the final restoration layers of HazeSpikeMamba without haze-free labels during adaptation. A shared checkpoint is adapted once on each complete unlabeled target set, making the evaluation dataset-level and transductive rather than zero-shot or per-image optimization. The forward network contains 2.02M active parameters and requires 13.27G nominal MACs (measured with thop at 256x256 input). This adaptation consistently improves BRISQUE and NIMA on RTTS, URHI, and HSTS. On RTTS, BRISQUE decreases from 30.13 to 27.72 and NIMA increases from 4.13 to 4.87. Under this transductive protocol, the adapted model also achieves the best BRISQUE and NIMA on URHI and HSTS among the compared methods.

</details>


### [119] [Prune Once: Retraining-Free Task-Agnostic Pruning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06901)
*Minseok Kang,Hyunwoo Kim,Chanyoung Kim,Minwoo Kim,Jaekoo Lee,Dahuin Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PORTA是一个免重训练、不依赖于目标任务样本的视觉-语言模型（VLM）剪枝框架，通过基于激活变化的任务无关重要性评估和自适应稀疏度分配机制，在CLIP、BLIP和Qwen2-VL等架构上实现了高稀疏度下具有竞争力的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在预训练后规模迅速增长，计算和内存开销巨大，难以部署在资源受限环境中。现有剪枝策略依赖任务特定样本或面向纯语言模型的剪枝准则，无法适用于任务无关剪枝（即在剪枝时没有任务样本可用且剪枝后模型需保持广泛适用性）的场景。

Method: PORTA提出两个核心组件：(1) 任务与模态无关的重要性评估公式，基于激活变化（activation variation），利用通用校准数据估计，可靠捕捉跨模态的特征级表征效用；(2) 自适应稀疏度分配机制，根据输出特征变异性确定各层剪枝比例，避免均匀稀疏带来的性能退化，尤其在高压缩比场景下。

Result: 在多个VLM架构（CLIP、BLIP、Qwen2-VL）上进行了广泛实验，结果表明PORTA在高稀疏度下取得具有竞争力的下游任务性能，且无需任何重训练（retraining-free），支持高效的VLM压缩。

Conclusion: PORTA通过任务无关的激活变化度量与自适应层间稀疏度分配，实现了免蒸馏、高压缩的VLM剪枝，为资源受限环境下的VLM部署提供了一种实用且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable generalization across diverse multimodal tasks through large-scale pre-training, yet their rapidly increasing computational and memory requirements pose significant challenges for deployment in constrained environments. Existing pruning strategies often depend on task-specific criteria or LLM-oriented importance measures, making them unsuitable for task-agnostic pruning, where no task-specific samples are available at pruning time and the pruned model remains broadly applicable. We introduce a retraining-free VLM pruning framework called PORTA that derives a task- and modality-agnostic importance formulation based on activation variation, estimated from generic calibration data, which reliably captures feature-level representation utility across modalities. PORTA further incorporates an adaptive sparsity allocation mechanism that assigns layer-wise pruning ratios based on output feature variability, avoiding the limitations of uniform sparsity and reducing performance degradation at high compression levels. Extensive experiments across VLM architectures, such as CLIP, BLIP, and Qwen2-VL, demonstrate that PORTA achieves competitive downstream performance under high sparsity without requiring any retraining, supporting efficient VLM compression. Code is available at https://github.com/cau-hai-lab/PORTA.git.

</details>


### [120] [MuST-VAD: Mutual Structured Learning for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06913)
*Satoshi Hashimoto,Hitoshi Nishimura,Mori Kurokawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MuST-VAD框架，将弱监督视频异常检测中从LVLM到检测器的单向特征迁移扩展为双向互学习循环：检测器预测监督LVLM适配，适配后的LVLM返回更新表征再训练检测器，两者交替更新。在UCF-Crime上AP提升至42.46%，超越SOTA 4.13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督VAD中，检测器从固定的任务无关骨干网络提取特征，这些固定特征限制了检测精度。现有方法将LVLM语义作为更丰富的特征迁移到检测器，但该迁移是单向的——检测器从目标视频中学到的知识从未回流到LVLM，造成信息利用不充分。

Method: MuST-VAD采用双向互学习循环：1) 检测器最新预测监督LVLM的适配过程；2) 适配后的LVLM返回更新表征用于重新训练检测器；3) 两个模型在小视频组上交替更新。关键设计包括：在检测器选定的关键片段上训练、置信度加权确保监督可靠性、注释锚定的问答机制保证交换监督的质量。

Result: 在UCF-Crime数据集上，MuST-VAD将单次迁移基线从88.15%提升至88.63% AUROC，平均精度从37.25%提升至42.46% AP，AP超越当前最先进方法4.13个百分点。

Conclusion: 双向互学习框架优于单向迁移，证明了检测器学到的任务特定知识回流到LVLM能形成正反馈循环，显著提升弱监督视频异常检测的性能，尤其在平均精度方面有大幅改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose MuST-VAD, a mutual structured learning framework for weakly supervised video anomaly detection (VAD) in which an anomaly detector and a large vision-language model (LVLM) exchange their acquired knowledge. Detectors in weakly supervised VAD learn anomaly scores from features extracted by a fixed, task-agnostic backbone. These fixed features bound the achievable detection accuracy. Recent methods therefore transfer LVLM semantics into the detector as richer features. However, this transfer is one-way: what the detector learns about the target videos never returns to the LVLM. MuST-VAD extends the one-way transfer into a bidirectional learning loop. In this loop, the latest detector predictions supervise the LVLM adaptation, and the adapted LVLM returns updated representations that retrain the detector; the two models alternate these updates over small video groups. Both models train on detector-selected key clips, while confidence weighting and annotation-anchored question answering keep the exchanged supervision reliable. On UCF-Crime, our mutual learning improves the one-pass transfer baseline from 88.15% to 88.63% AUROC and from 37.25% to 42.46% average precision (AP), outperforming the state-of-the-art method in AP by 4.13 points.

</details>


### [121] [RibAssist 3D: Biplanar Rib-Fracture Detection, Addressing, and Selective 3D Localization from CT-Derived Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06914)
*Kabila Haile Soboka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双平面（前后位AP与侧位）几何重建的肋骨骨折三维定位方法。通过将两个正交投影中检测到的骨折中心配对联立，可在受控的假阳性率下实现可靠的3D骨折点重建。研究采用分阶段诊断试验设计，发现关键瓶颈并非几何或定位精度，而是跨视图对应的置信度受限。通过侧位探测器重训练提升了双视图可用率，最终在密封队列上以每例0.436个假3D点的代价，将15/601处骨折提升为正确3D定位，端到端承诺产出率为2.50%。该贡献是验证了双平面重建几何的高条件定位保真度、识别出跨视图对应置信度是有效瓶颈，并提出一个保留不确定发现的辅助工作流而非独立自动重建器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肋骨骨折在CT上定位常见且耗时，作者希望解答一个问题：在两个正交投影（AP与侧位）中检测到的骨折能否跨视图配对并三角化为可靠的3D骨折点，同时在受控的假3D输出率下实现。

Method: 采用分阶段诊断研究设计。利用双平面几何（biplanar geometry）进行精确重建：当对应关系正确时，检测器预测的中心可重建为4.0mm中位3D误差。在密封队列上评估双视图可用率与候选图正确配对比例。对侧位探测器进行重训练以提升双视图可用率，并移动前端（frontier）。在未触碰的55例队列上执行预设的通过策略（pass），将骨折提升为正确3D定位，并统计假3D点数量。

Result: 双平面几何精确：对应关系正确时重建中位3D误差为4.0mm。密封队列上双视图可用率达61.1%，候选图中58.4%的骨折包含正确配对。限制瓶颈是置信度受限的跨视图对应而非几何或定位。侧位探测器重训练使双视图可用率从0.52提升至0.76（开发集），前沿从0%移至2.44%（10mm召回率）。当策略提交正确配对时，发出点的几何精度高（密封中位1.49mm，肋骨精确93%）。在55例未触碰队列上，预设pass将601处骨折中的15处提升为正确3D定位，每例0.436个假3D点，端到端承诺产出率为2.50%。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于：验证了双平面重建几何具有高条件定位保真度；通过分阶段识别确定跨视图对应置信度是有效瓶颈；提出了一种选择性辅助工作流，该工作流保留不确定发现，而非构建独立的自动重建器。

Abstract: Rib fractures are common, clinically significant, and time-consuming to localize on computed tomography (CT). We ask whether fractures detected in two orthogonal projections (anteroposterior, AP, and lateral) can be paired across views and triangulated into reliable 3D fracture points at a controlled rate of false 3D outputs. We answer this with a staged diagnostic study. Biplanar geometry is exact: detector-predicted centers reconstruct to median 4.0 mm 3D error when correspondence is correct. On the sealed cohort, dual-view availability reaches 61.1% and the candidate graph contains a correct pair for 58.4% of fractures. The binding limitation is not geometry or localization but confidence-limited cross-view correspondence. Lateral-detector retraining lifts dual-view availability (0.52 to 0.76 in development) and moves the frontier from 0% to 2.44% recall at 10 mm. When the policy commits a correct pair, the emitted point is geometrically accurate (sealed median 1.49 mm, rib-exact 93%). A pre-specified pass on the untouched 55-case cohort promotes 15 of 601 fractures to correct 3D localizations at 0.436 false 3D points per case, yielding 2.50% end-to-end commitment yield. The contribution is validated biplanar reconstruction geometry with high conditional localization fidelity, a staged identification of cross-view correspondence confidence as the effective bottleneck, and a selective assistive workflow that preserves uncertain findings rather than a standalone automatic reconstructor.

</details>


### [122] [Vernata: Self-Supervised Learning of LiDAR Point Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06919)
*Oliver Lemke,Alexander Liniger,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vernata，一个基于Sonata架构的多模态多教师蒸馏框架，用于户外LiDAR点云的自监督学习，通过稀疏视图增强、记忆库机制和跨模态蒸馏三项扩展，在多个数据集上显著超越基线，并展示了在缺失颜色或法线等降模态设置下仍保持竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外环境中LiDAR是机器人的主要感知模态，但3D标注成本高昂导致有标签数据稀缺，严重制约了深度学习模型的性能。自监督学习可以从未标注数据中学习通用特征以缓解数据稀缺问题，但现有方法在户外LiDAR点云上的表现仍有提升空间。

Method: Vernata建立在Sonata架构之上，包含三个核心扩展：(1) 稀疏视图增强——提升对点云密度变化的鲁棒性；(2) 记忆库机制——稳定资源受限条件下的训练过程；(3) 跨模态蒸馏——利用密集高分辨率的2D图像特征提供细粒度的语义引导，实现多模态多教师的蒸馏学习。

Result: 在GrandTour、TartanGround和Waymo数据集及自建机器人平台数据上评估，Vernata显著超越Sonata基线：TartanGround上mIoU达54.7（+5.9点，+12.1%），Waymo上达57.1（+7.3点，+14.7%）。在缺乏颜色或法线的降模态设置下，仍分别取得49.4和50.2的竞争性mIoU分数。

Conclusion: 多模态多教师蒸馏框架Vernata有效提升了户外LiDAR点云自监督学习的性能，且自监督方法即使在降模态（缺少颜色或法线）条件下仍保持较强性能，证明了该方法的鲁棒性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: LiDAR serves as a primary sensing modality for robots operating in outdoor environments. However, the performance of deep learning models in this domain is severely limited by the scarcity of labeled data, a direct result of the high cost of 3D annotation. Self-supervised learning addresses this scarcity by learning general-purpose features from unlabeled data. In this work, we present a multi-modal, multi-teacher distillation framework for self-supervised learning on outdoor LiDAR point clouds. Building upon the Sonata architecture, we introduce Vernata, consisting of three extensions: sparse view augmentation to improve robustness against varying point densities, a memory bank mechanism to stabilize resource-constrained training, and cross-modal distillation utilizing dense, high-resolution 2D image features to enable fine-grained semantic guidance. We evaluate our method on the GrandTour, TartanGround, and Waymo datasets, as well as data collected from our own robotic platforms. Our experiments demonstrate a significant performance improvement over Sonata baselines, yielding mIoU scores of 54.7 on TartanGround (+5.9 points, +12.1%) and 57.1 on Waymo (+7.3 points, +14.7%). Finally, we show that the self-supervised approach maintains strong performance even in reduced-modality settings (lacking color or normals), achieving competitive mIoU scores of 49.4 and 50.2 on the respective datasets.

</details>


### [123] [MaskFlow: Precise, Consistent and Seamless Regional Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06929)
*Rui Xu,Yang Yong,Shunzi Yang,Ruihao Gong,Chengtao Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MaskFlow，一个用于区域图像编辑的训练框架，通过将掩码集成到概率路径和流匹配目标中实现精确定位、背景一致保留和无缝边界过渡；同时提出 Soft-Poisson 去缝模块优化向量场，并构建了掩码图像编辑数据集 MEData，在自然场景和信息图表图像上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域图像编辑虽已引起广泛关注，指令式和掩码参考式方法能够实现较强的语义对齐，但可靠的区域控制仍然具有挑战性——编辑需要被精确定位，且必须与保留的背景自然地融合。现有的基于掩码的方法在编辑区域的定位准确性和生成区域与未编辑区域的边界过渡一致性方面仍存在不足。

Method: MaskFlow 是一个基于流匹配（flow matching）的训练框架，其核心创新包括：（1）将掩码直接集成到概率路径和流匹配目标中，在可编辑区域内协调生成，同时在区域外保持源图像内容；（2）提出 Soft-Poisson 去缝模块，在训练和采样阶段均对预测的向量场进行细化，以改善编辑前景与保留背景之间的平滑融合；（3）设计数据合成流程构建 MEData 数据集，用于训练区域编辑模型。

Result: 在自然场景和信息图表图像上的实验表明，MaskFlow 在定量和定性评估中均相较于对比方法取得了一致的性能提升，验证了其在区域编辑定位精度、背景一致性和边界融合效果方面的优势。

Conclusion: MaskFlow 通过将掩码信息融入流匹配框架并引入 Soft-Poisson 去缝机制，有效解决了区域图像编辑中精确定位、背景保留与无缝边界这三个核心挑战，并贡献了 MEData 数据集以推动该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Regional image editing has attracted considerable attention for its spatial controllability. Although instruction-based and mask-reference-based editing methods can achieve strong semantic alignment, reliable regional control remains challenging, where an edit must be accurately localized and naturally integrated with the preserved context. We propose \textbf{MaskFlow}, a training framework for precise localization, consistent background preservation, and seamless boundary transitions. MaskFlow incorporates the mask into the probability path and flow-matching objective, coordinating generation within the editable region with source preservation outside it. The proposed Soft-Poisson de-seaming module further refines the predicted vector field during both training and sampling to improve the smooth integration of the edited foreground with the preserved background. We also design a data synthesis pipeline to construct MEData, a mask-based image editing dataset for training regional image editing models and facilitating further research. Experiments on natural scenes and infographic images demonstrate consistent improvements over competing methods in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations.

</details>


### [124] [AVCap: Reinforcing Audio-Video Joint Caption with Detail-Aware Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06930)
*Mingyang Wu,Kaituo Feng,Bohao Li,Kaixiong Gong,Zihao Yin,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套完整的音频-视频联合字幕生成解决方案，包括AVCap-100K数据集、基于Detail-Aware GRPO优化的AVCap模型，以及AVCap-Bench与AVCap-Score测评基准和指标，旨在解决细粒度音视频联合字幕生成中数据稀缺、奖励信号粗糙和评测缺失三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度音频-视频联合字幕对于多模态视频理解和生成至关重要，但现有工作受限于三大瓶颈：(1) 高质量公开数据集稀缺，缺乏细粒度音频-视觉联合标注；(2) 强化学习方法依赖粗糙的奖励信号，难以捕捉原子级细节；(3) 缺乏针对音视频字幕原子级细节的专用基准和评测指标。

Method: 提出三部分解决方案：(1) 构建AVCap-100K，包含10万条时间对齐、细节丰富的音视频字幕数据集；(2) 提出AVCap模型，采用Detail-Aware GRPO（Da-GRPO）优化策略，通过细粒度细节感知的奖励信号进行强化学习；(3) 构建AVCap-Bench基准与AVCap-Score指标，用于原子级音视频字幕细节的评测。

Result: AVCap模型在开源模型中达到最优性能，在多项评测中达到或超越专有闭源模型；AVCap-Bench和AVCap-Score提供了对音视频字幕原子级细节的可靠评测能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过数据集、模型和评测体系三位一体的贡献，系统性地解决了音视频联合字幕生成中长期存在的数据稀缺、奖励粗糙和评测缺失问题，推动了细粒度多模态视频理解与生成的发展。

Abstract: Detailed audio-video joint captioning is essential for multimodal video understanding and generation. However, prior works are constrained by three main limitations: (1) the scarcity of high-quality public datasets with fine-grained audio-visual joint captions; (2) reinforcement-learning methods that rely on coarse reward signals; and (3) the lack of a benchmark and metric for evaluating detailed audiovisual captions at the atomic level. To address these challenges, we propose: (1) AVCap-100K, a high-quality dataset of 100K temporally aligned, detail-rich audio-video captions; (2) AVCap, a model optimized via Detail-Aware GRPO (Da-GRPO) that achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models and matches or surpasses proprietary models on several evaluations; and (3) AVCap-Bench and AVCap-Score, a specialized benchmark and metric for evaluating atomic-level details in audiovisual captions. Our code, models, and datasets are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/Apryle/avcap.

</details>


### [125] [Debias in Text, Believe Your Eyes: Text-Anchored Cross-Modal Transfer for Visual Counter-Commonsense Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06938)
*Chen Ling,Hanqian Li,Dongnan Liu,Keyu Qian,Jungang Li,Xinglong liu,Shiyi Wang,Xin Dong,Pengcheng Zhu,Wei Zhou,Linjian Mo,Nai Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在反常识视觉推理中的失败瓶颈并非视觉感知不足，而是共享语言解码器偏好语言先验、忽视视觉证据所致。作者提出文本锚定数据构建管线（含事实频率蒸馏FFD）生成高质量反常识文本语料，并在此基础上提出TACT文本锚定后训练框架，通过分离证据跟随与先验驱动推理轨迹来消除解码器偏见，在无需任何视觉训练数据的情况下显著提升反常识视觉推理性能并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要假设MLLM在反常识视觉推理上的失败源于视觉输入不足，因此通过增强视觉输入来改进。然而作者的经验分析表明，模型其实已经捕获了相关视觉证据，正确回答也存在于解码空间中，真正的瓶颈在于共享语言解码器在证据与先验冲突时倾向于固守主导性语言先验（尤其对低频事实场景）。这推翻了既有假设，促使作者从文本锚定角度解决解码器偏差。

Method: 本文提出两条核心组件：（1）文本锚定数据构建管线，其核心是事实频率蒸馏（FFD），用于估计常识事实的先验强度并蒸馏出经过验证的反常识场景，构建高质量文本语料库；（2）TACT文本锚定后训练框架，将证据跟随型与先验驱动型推理轨迹分配到不同优化阶段，使共享语言解码器学会正确解决先验-证据冲突。整个过程无需任何视觉训练数据。

Result: 在多个反常识视觉基准上，TACT显著提升了视觉推理性能，同时保持模型通用能力不下降，证明了文本到视觉的跨模态迁移的有效性。

Conclusion: 反常识视觉推理的瓶颈不在视觉感知而在于语言解码器的先验偏差；通过文本锚定的后训练方式去偏解码器，可实现无需视觉数据的跨模态推理能力迁移，为MLLM反常识推理提供了一种全新且高效的技术路线。

Abstract: The visual reasoning ability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is crucial for downstream applications, particularly counter-commonsense reasoning, which requires models to reason beyond common assumptions. Recent studies mainly improve visual counter-commonsense reasoning by enhancing visual inputs, following the assumption that failures originate from insufficient visual grounding. However, our empirical analysis reveals that the bottleneck is not visual perception. MLLMs already capture the relevant visual evidence, and the correct answer exists in their decoding space. Instead, the shared language decoder resolves prior--evidence conflicts by favoring dominant language priors, especially for low-frequency factual scenarios. Motivated by this, we first propose a text-anchored data construction pipeline, whose core component, Fact-Frequency Distillation (FFD), estimates the prior strength of commonsense facts and distills verified counter-commonsense scenarios into a high-quality text corpus. Building upon this corpus, we introduce TACT, a text-anchored post-training framework that debiases the shared language decoder without requiring any visual training data. TACT routes evidence-following and prior-driven reasoning trajectories into different optimization stages, enabling the decoder to resolve prior--evidence conflicts. Across counter-commonsense visual benchmarks, TACT substantially improves visual reasoning while preserving general capabilities, demonstrating effective text-to-vision cross-modal transfer.

</details>


### [126] [Degradation-Aware Prompt Learning with Cross-Modal Compensation for Adverse Weather Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06939)
*Wanshu Fan,Yunzhe Zhang,Yue Shen,Liyan Wang,Jing Qin,Kin-Man Lam,Cong Wang,Jinshan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCMPC-Net，利用预训练视觉-语言模型的跨模态退化线索，通过三个模块（CMPG、PGAAM、DFCM）在统一骨干网络中实现多种恶劣天气条件下的图像恢复，性能优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全能图像恢复模型虽然能在统一框架中处理多种退化类型，但缺乏对退化特征的显式空间和语义建模，导致对不同天气条件的适应性不足。

Method: DCMPC-Net包含三个核心组件：1) 跨模态提示生成器（CMPG），融合文本嵌入与视觉特征生成退化感知提示；2) 提示引导注意力对齐模块（PGAAM），将语义信息与退化区域自适应对齐；3) 双特征补偿模块（DFCM），解耦退化伪影与场景结构以增强结构保真度。

Result: 在任务特定和统一设置下均超越现有最先进方法，实现了更高的恢复精度和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 通过整合跨模态语义引导、空间对齐和结构增强，DCMPC-Net能够在多种天气条件下实现鲁棒且感知一致的图像恢复，验证了跨模态退化感知建模对提升统一图像恢复性能的有效性。

Abstract: Adverse weather causes diverse and complex image degradations, severely compromising the reliability of computer vision systems. Existing all-in-one restoration models attempt to address multiple degradation types within a unified framework, but often lack explicit spatial and semantic modeling of degradation characteristics, limiting their adaptability to diverse weather conditions. To address this limitation, we propose a Degradation-Aware Cross-Modal Prompt Compensation Network (DCMPC-Net) that leverages cross-modal degradation cues from a pretrained vision-language model to condition restoration features within a unified backbone. Specifically, our DCMPC-Net mainly consists of the Cross-Modal Prompt Generator (CMPG), Prompt-Guided Attention Alignment Module (PGAAM), and Dual Feature Compensation Module (DFCM). The CMPG integrates textual embeddings with visual features to produce degradation-aware prompts that encode degradation-related semantic and contextual cues. These prompts are injected into the decoder via a PGAAM, which adaptively aligns semantic information with degraded regions to facilitate context-aware restoration. To further enhance structural fidelity, DFCM is introduced that disentangles degradation artifacts from scene structures, thereby improving the reconstruction of fine textures and detailed content. By integrating cross-modal semantic guidance with spatial alignment and structural enhancement, DCMPC-Net achieves robust and perceptually consistent restoration across diverse weather conditions. Extensive experiments show that DCMPC-Net outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both task-specific and unified settings, achieving superior accuracy and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [127] [Dual-Space Modality Consistency Learning for Universal Cross-Modal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06943)
*Yujian Zhao,Yukang Zhao,Hankun Liu,Haoxuan Xu,Bo Li,Hanzi Wan,Guanglin Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个名为DSMCL（Dual-Space Modality Consistency Learning）的通用跨模态行人重识别框架，通过在空间域和频域双空间联合建模模态一致性（空间特征分布一致性+频域判别一致性），能够即插即用地集成到现有跨模态ReID架构中，在五个基准数据集、十七个评估协议上一致提升多个代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态ReID方法虽在空间嵌入空间学习模态一致性取得不错效果，但忽略了频域中的模态差异，尤其是高频率表示既具有高度判别性又对模态敏感。此外，大多数方法针对特定模态设置设计，限制了其在多样化跨模态场景（如可见光-红外行人ReID与跨模态船舶ReID）中的通用性。

Method: 提出DSMCL框架：（1）空间模态一致性学习分支（SMCL）通过基于高斯分布的特征对齐保持空间域特征分布一致性；（2）频域判别一致性学习策略（FDCL）通过身份感知的跨模态对比学习来正则化高频率表示；（3）通过联合捕获模态特定特征与模态共享的身份线索，学习鲁棒表示并构建统一框架；该框架为即插即用设计，可方便集成到现有跨模态ReID架构中。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01、RegDB、LLCM、HOSS-ReID和CMShipReID五个跨模态ReID基准数据集上进行广泛实验，覆盖十七个评估协议，结果表明DSMCL在多个代表性基线上均实现一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过同时建模空间域的分布一致性与频域的判别一致性，DSMCL有效解决了跨模态ReID中频域模态差异被忽视的问题，并以即插即用的通用框架在多种跨模态场景中展现出广泛的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Cross-modal Re-Identification (ReID) aims to retrieve the same identity across heterogeneous imaging modalities and has been widely studied in visible-infrared person ReID and cross-modal ship ReID. Existing methods have achieved promising performance by learning modality consistency in the spatial embedding space, yet often overlook frequency-domain modality discrepancy, particularly in high-frequency representations that are both highly discriminative and modality-sensitive. In addition, most approaches are tailored to specific modality settings, limiting their applicability across diverse cross-modal scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose a Dual-Space Modality Consistency Learning (DSMCL) framework for universal cross-modal ReID. Specifically, DSMCL jointly models spatial feature distribution consistency and frequency-domain discriminative consistency. A Spatial Modality Consistency Learning (SMCL) branch performs Gaussian-based feature alignment, while a Frequency-aware Discriminative Consistency Learning (FDCL) strategy regularizes high-frequency representations through identity-aware cross-modal contrastive learning. By jointly capturing modality-specific characteristics and modality-shared identity cues, DSMCL learns robust representations and establishes a unified framework capable of accommodating diverse heterogeneous modality settings. Moreover, DSMCL is a plug-and-play framework that can be readily integrated into existing cross-modal ReID architectures. Extensive experiments on SYSU-MM01, RegDB, LLCM, HOSS-ReID, and CMShipReID across seventeen evaluation protocols show that DSMCL consistently improves multiple representative baselines.

</details>


### [128] [Summarize First, Download Later: Onboard VLMs for Bandwidth-Efficient Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06959)
*Junghwan Park,Sangcheol Sim,Woojin Cho,Darongsae Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先摘要、后下载"范式，利用机载边缘计算和量化视觉-语言模型（VLM），让卫星先生成自然语言摘要、地面操作员通过VQA查询验证场景相关性，仅在确认关键信息时才下载全分辨率图像，从而大幅降低带宽消耗并加速决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代对地观测卫星传感器能力日益增强，产生海量高分辨率多光谱数据，但下行链路带宽成为关键瓶颈，在有限联系窗口内常造成显著延迟或高价值观测数据丢失。

Method: 采用"Summarize First, Download Later"三阶段交互协议：(1)卫星通过量化机载VLM生成简洁自然语言摘要并回传；(2)地面操作员发送针对性VQA查询以验证场景相关性（如野火或海上异常）；(3)仅当确认关键信息时下载全分辨率图像。系统在资源受限的NVIDIA Jetson平台实现。

Result: 在多种遥感场景上的实验表明，该策略在显著降低带宽消耗的同时，加速了时间敏感任务的信息获取速度（time-to-insight）。

Conclusion: 该工作将卫星下行链路从被动批量传输转变为主动的、语义感知的对话机制，为未来智能对地观测系统中的带宽高效利用提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Modern Earth observation (EO) satellites carry increasingly advanced sensors that produce vast volumes of high-resolution, multispectral data, yet downlink capacity remains a critical bottleneck -- often causing significant latency or the loss of valuable observations within limited contact windows. We propose a "Summarize First, Download Later" paradigm that exploits recent advances in onboard edge computing and Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Rather than indiscriminately downlinking raw imagery, the system follows a three-phase interaction protocol: the satellite first transmits concise natural language summaries generated by a quantized onboard VLM; ground operators then issue targeted Visual Question Answering (VQA) queries to verify scene relevance (e.g., wildfires or maritime anomalies); and full-resolution images are downloaded only when critical information is confirmed. This transforms the downlink from passive bulk transfer into an active, semantics-aware dialogue. We implement and evaluate the system on a resource-constrained NVIDIA Jetson platform, and experiments on diverse remote sensing scenes show that the proposed strategy substantially reduces bandwidth consumption while accelerating time-to-insight for time-sensitive missions.

</details>


### [129] [Generative Embedding Benchmark: How Much Information Survives in a Dense Embedding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06972)
*Yun Li,Biao Yang,Peixi Wu,Yunhao Zhou,Mingzhou Jiang,Wei Yuan,Fan Yang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了生成式嵌入基准（GEB），通过让解码器仅凭冻结嵌入和问题文本回答问题来评估嵌入中可恢复的生成信息，发现基于可分性的传统基准无法捕捉的信息瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入基准主要通过判别式任务或几何可分性标准评估表示质量，但这些评估无法证明嵌入中压缩的内容是否真正可供下游生成器访问。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建GEB基准，包含1,800项开发集和900项测试集的视觉问答数据集，覆盖自然图像、场景文本和视觉文档。解码器仅使用冻结嵌入和问题文本回答问题，评估7个公开嵌入模型的视觉单模态和视觉-语言联合两种模式，并设置多种对照（文本输入、零嵌入、乱序嵌入）。

Result: 测试集上视觉单模态得分为28.25–33.21；加入图像-问题联合编码后，5个VLM嵌入模型均表现更好，最佳达65.56。匹配嵌入优于文本输入、零嵌入和乱序嵌入。自然图像信息比场景文本或视觉文档更易恢复，而访问原始图像的Qwen3-VL-2B参考模型达到84.30。

Conclusion: 生成式读出揭示了基于可分性评估无法捕捉的信息瓶颈，表明现有嵌入评估方法可能高估了嵌入在下游生成任务中的可用性。

Abstract: Embeddings have emerged as a standard representational interface linking foundation models with downstream systems. Most embedding benchmarks assess representations through discriminative tasks or geometric criteria centered on separability in embedding space. However, strong performance on such evaluations does not establish whether content compressed into an embedding remains accessible to a downstream generator. To address this gap, we introduce the Generative Embedding Benchmark (GEB), in which a decoder answers questions using only a frozen embedding and question text, without access to the original image or intermediate visual features. Answer quality under this readout measures generative information: the answer-relevant content recoverable from an embedding. GEB includes a curated visual-question-answering dataset with a 1,800-item development split and a held-out 900-item test split covering natural images, scene text, and visual documents. Using a common decoder and training recipe, we evaluate seven public embedding models in visual-only and vision-language joint modes. On the test set, visual-only scores range from 28.25 to 33.21; with image-question joint encoding, all five VLM-based embedding models score higher, and the best reaches 65.56. Matched embeddings also outperform text-only inputs, zero embeddings, and shuffled embeddings. Natural-image information is much easier to recover than scene text or visual-document information, while a Qwen3-VL-2B reference with access to the original image reaches 84.30.
